### [86] [Inertial Asynchronous Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21965)
*Doruk Efe Gökmen,Michel Fruchart,Dmitrii Zendrikov,Giacomo Indiveri,Giulio Biroli,Vincenzo Vitelli*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过将计算硬件拆分为两个非对称耦合部分（类似简谐振子的位置与动量），利用产生的惯性推动异步多体系统的状态演化，使异步计算（asynchronous computation）能够按正确顺序进行。研究结合非平衡材料视角、环动力学平均场理论（loop dynamical mean-field theory）以及模拟脉冲神经形态芯片（analog spiking neuromorphic chips）实验，展示了可靠去中心化计算（decentralized computation）在能量受限场景中的通用策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然过程如何在无全局协调（global orchestration）的情况下，利用大量单元执行异步演化（asynchronous evolutions），是一个尚未解决的根本性难题。现有计算框架依赖同步控制，无法解释或复现这种自发的集体计算能力。

Method: 将物理硬件分为两个非对称耦合部分，类似简谐振子的位置（position）与动量（momentum），利用惯性推动状态演化。将异步惯性计算机视为非平衡材料（nonequilibrium material），通过环动力学平均场理论（loop dynamical mean-field theory）进行数值和解析相图（phase diagram）分析。使用模拟脉冲神经形态芯片（analog spiking neuromorphic chips）进行实验验证，并构建可在异步硬件上运行的软件。

Result: 在神经形态芯片上实验验证了异步计算方法的可行性。软件方面，成功实现从训练时未见过的干净版本中给电影去噪（denoise movies），这是现代机器学习泛化转变（generalization transition）的一个实例。

Conclusion: 研究结果为在能量受限场景中实现可靠、去中心化计算（decentralized computation）提供了通用策略，适用于从动力学自组装（dynamics self-assembly）到细胞分化（cell differentiation）等自然过程。

Abstract: Computation is the controlled evolution of a state. Asynchronous evolutions, where all parts of the state change in their own time without stopping each other, put this control in jeopardy. It is in fact a mystery how natural processes perform asynchronous computations using many units with no global orchestration. Here we demonstrate how collective computational abilities can emerge in asynchronous many-body systems. The key insight is to split the physical "hardware" underlying the computation into two asymmetrically coupled parts, analogous to position and momentum in a harmonic oscillator. The resulting inertia nudges the evolution of the state so that the asynchronous computation proceeds in the right order. By treating our inertial asynchronous computer as a nonequilibrium material, we map out its phase diagram numerically and analytically using a framework we dub loop dynamical mean-field theory. We experimentally demonstrate our approach using analog spiking neuromorphic chips designed to mimic actual neurons in the brain. In addition, we construct software that can run on asynchronous hardware: we denoise movies whose clean versions were never seen during training, an instantiation of the generalization transition underlying modern machine learning. Our results point to a general strategy for reliable, decentralized computation in energy-constrained settings from dynamics self-assembly to cell differentiation.

</details>


### [87] [Extreme First-Passage Time of Many Interacting Particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22528)
*Ruicheng Bao*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文针对相互作用的极端首通事件（extreme first-passage events）建立了一个通用理论框架。通过一个“no-go”定理证明，有界相互作用无法超越独立布朗搜索者的 $1/\ln N$ 极端时间尺度；同时识别出两种超越对数类（logarithmic class）的尖锐机制，并推导出统一的相互作用驱动加速极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有极端统计（extreme statistics）理论局限于非相互作用搜索者，而相互作用情形由于关联性破坏概率因式分解（probability factorization）而难以处理。本文旨在建立适用于相互作用系统的极端搜索通用框架。

Method: 通过建立通用理论框架，首先证明一个“no-go”定理，指出广泛类别的有界相互作用无法超越独立布朗搜索者的 $1/\ln N$ 极端时间尺度；再通过互补上界证明该尺度对广泛类别的排斥相互作用是精确的。随后识别出两种超越对数类的机制，并导出统一的相互作用驱动加速极限（interaction-driven acceleration limit）。

Result: 确定性成对相互作用（deterministic pairwise interaction）最多将极端搜索时间降至 $1/N$ 量级，而随机成对强迫（stochastic pairwise forcing）可达到 $1/(N\ln N)$ 量级。这些结果区分了由统计冗余（statistical redundancy）产生的加速与由相干多体输运（coherent many-body transport）或放大涨落（amplified fluctuations）产生的加速。

Conclusion: 该工作加深了对相互作用随机系统（interacting stochastic systems）的理解，阐明了不同加速机制的来源及其极限。

Abstract: Extreme first-passage events are broadly relevant to biological, chemical, and physical processes in which the first successful arrival determines the outcome. Existing theories are confined to noninteracting searchers. Interacting extreme-statistics problems are notoriously difficult because correlations destroy probability factorization. We establish a general framework for interacting extreme search. A no-go theorem shows that broad classes of bounded interactions cannot beat the $1/\ln N$ extreme timescale of $N$ independent Brownian searchers, and complementary upper bounds prove that this scale is exact for broad classes of repulsive interactions. We then identify two sharp mechanisms beyond the logarithmic class and derive a unified interaction-driven acceleration limit. In particular, deterministic pairwise interaction can at most reduce the extreme search time to order $1/N$, while stochastic pairwise forcing attains $1/(N\ln N)$. Our results separate acceleration due to statistical redundancy from that generated by coherent many-body transport or amplified fluctuations, deepening our understanding of interacting stochastic systems.

</details>


### [88] [Fermions on a 1D lattice: localized sources and sinks with dephasing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22240)
*Elka Bhattacharya,Sushant Saryal,Soumya Ghosh,Kabir Ramola*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究受局域源、汇和体退相干（bulk dephasing）作用的一维无自旋费米子（spinless fermionic）系统。在Lindblad框架下，发现体退相干会抑制Friedel振荡等量子相干特征，并将输运动力学从三种不同的动力学区域简化为两种，强退相干下系统趋近于经典扩散（classical diffusive）行为。此外，源-汇分离距离的整数倍处会出现二级密度峰（secondary density peaks），这些峰随退相干增强而被系统性地消除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解体退相干（bulk dephasing）如何影响一维无自旋费米子系统的量子相干特性和输运动力学，以及该系统如何从量子行为过渡到经典扩散（classical diffusive）行为。

Method: 采用Lindblad框架（Lindblad formalism）描述开放量子系统的动力学，数值计算密度分布（density profile）和空间关联函数（spatial correlation functions）的时间演化。

Result: 体退相干（bulk dephasing）抑制了Friedel振荡（Friedel oscillations）等量子相干特征；输运动力学从无退相干时的三种动力学区域简化为两种；强退相干下密度分布类似于经典扩散（classical diffusive）行为；在局域源和汇同时存在时，源-汇分离距离的整数倍处出现二级密度峰（secondary density peaks），这些峰随退相干增强而消失。

Conclusion: 体退相干（bulk dephasing）通过破坏弹道运动（ballistic motion）将系统从量子相干输运推向经典扩散（classical diffusive）极限，这为理解开放量子系统中退相干导致量子–经典转变（quantum-to-classical transition）提供了新视角。

Abstract: We study a general one-dimensional spinless fermionic system subject to a localized source, sink, and bulk dephasing. Within the Lindblad framework, we compute the time evolution of the density profile and spatial correlation functions. We find that the presence of bulk dephasing suppresses certain coherent quantum features, such as the Friedel oscillations, and it alters the transport dynamics to exhibit two distinct dynamical regimes instead of three as observed in the absence of dephasing. This effect can be understood as the destruction of ballistic motion caused by the dephasing noise. Under strong dephasing, the density profile becomes similar to the one expected for a classical diffusive regime. We also investigate the system in the presence of both a localized source and sink, placed at a distance of $Δ$. Interestingly, quantum coherence generates secondary density peaks at integer multiples of the source-sink separation $Δ$, which are systematically washed out as bulk dephasing drives the system toward the classical diffusive limit.

</details>

### [89] [Turbulence in Quantum Gases: Vortices, Waves, and Cascades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22244)
*Ashton S. Bradley,Tyler W. Neely,Xiaoquan Yu,Brian P. Anderson*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文综述了超冷量子气体中的湍流现象，以标量接触相互作用玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体作为参考系统，系统介绍了连接氦湍流和经典现象学与微观波函数动力学的定量诊断方法，并回顾了相关实验进展及更广泛的量子流体普适性前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为超冷量子气体湍流领域提供一个系统性的定量诊断框架，弥合经典湍流现象学与微观波函数动力学之间的鸿沟，并澄清仅凭幂律(power law)不足以证明湍流级联(cascade)存在的关键问题。

Method: 采用标量接触相互作用玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体(Bose-Einstein condensate)作为参考系统，利用不可压缩与可压缩动能谱(incompressible and compressible kinetic-energy spectra)、波占据谱(wave-occupation spectra)、谱通量(spectral fluxes)、涡旋分辨关联(vortex-resolved correlations)和速度统计(velocity statistics)等定量诊断方法，连接氦湍流与经典现象学。

Result: 这些定量诊断能够区分平衡涡旋组织(equilibrium vortex organization)、衰减湍流弛豫(decaying turbulent relaxation)、受迫级联动力学(forced cascade dynamics)和弱波湍流(weak-wave turbulence)；实验方面涵盖了二维Onsager聚类、三维涡线湍流、盒状势阱波级联、工程耗散及湍流状态方程(turbulent equations of state)等研究。

Conclusion: 接触相互作用的标量超流体系统可作为测试湍流普适性(universality)的基准平台，未来可向非局域、多组分、费米子和驱动-耗散量子流体等更广泛的量子流体系统延伸。

Abstract: We review turbulence in ultracold quantum gases, using the scalar contact-interaction Bose-Einstein condensate as the reference system for quantized circulation, compressibility, vortices, sound, and cascades. We focus on the quantitative diagnostics that connect helium and classical phenomenology to microscopic wave-function dynamics: incompressible and compressible kinetic-energy spectra, wave-occupation spectra, spectral fluxes, vortex-resolved correlations, and velocity statistics. These diagnostics distinguish equilibrium vortex organization, decaying turbulent relaxation, forced cascade dynamics, and weak-wave turbulence, and show why power laws alone are insufficient evidence for a cascade. We survey experiments on two-dimensional Onsager clustering, three-dimensional vortex-line turbulence, box-trap wave cascades, engineered dissipation, and turbulent equations of state. We close by briefly placing the contact-interaction scalar superfluid system in a broader landscape of nonlocal, multicomponent, fermionic, and driven-dissipative quantum fluids, where turbulence concepts can be tested for universality.

</details>

### [90] [Performance of a first multi-cell WOM-based liquid scintillator detector as prototype for the SHiP Surrounding Background Tagger](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22477)
*M. Böhles,A. Brignoli,P. Deucher,C. Eckardt,H. Fischer,A. Hollnagel,A. Krolla,H. Lacker,P. Luther,F. Lyons,J. Molins i Bertram,T. Molzberger,A. S. Müller,S. Ochoa Guaman,F. Rehbein,T. J. Rock,M. Schaaf,C. Scharf,M. Schumann,J. M. Webb,J. Wenk,I. Wöstheinrich,M. Wurm*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 本文报告了SHiP实验的环绕背景标记器（SBT）全尺寸2x2单元原型探测器在CERN PS T9测试束流设施中接受5GeV缪子照射的性能测试结果，重点研究了探测器对最小电离粒子的响应、时间分辨率及空间分辨率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SHiP实验需要一个密封的环绕背景标记器（SBT）来包裹50米长的衰变体积（Decay Volume），以探测弱相互作用粒子（feebly-interacting particles）并抑制背景噪声。

Method: 采用液体闪烁体（liquid scintillator）作为活性探测器材料，由线性烷基苯（linear alkylbenzene）和2,5-二苯基噁唑（2,5-diphenyloxazole）组成；使用波长位移光学模块（Wavelength-shifting Optical Module, WOM）管收集初级闪烁光子；液体闪烁体体积被分割为120cm x 80cm x 20cm的大型单元，每个单元配备两个WOM。

Result: 在CERN PS T9测试束流设施中使用5GeV缪子对全尺寸2x2单元原型探测器进行了性能测试，获得了探测器响应、时间分辨率和空间分辨率的数据。

Conclusion: 该原型测试为SHiP实验SBT探测器的设计验证和性能评估提供了实验依据，表明该技术方案可用于最小电离粒子在多个探测器单元中穿越时的信号探测。

Abstract: The Search for Hidden Particles (SHiP) Experiment was approved by CERN in 2024. Feebly-interacting particles that are produced in a proton Beam Dump Facility (BDF) will decay in the 50m-long Decay Volume of the experiment, which needs to be enveloped by a hermetic veto detector: The Surrounding Background Tagger (SBT). Its technology relies on liquid scintillator, composed of linear alkylbenzene and 2,5-diphenyloxazole, as active detector material and Wavelength-shifting Optical Module (WOM) tubes collecting the primary scintillation photons. The liquid scintillator volume is segmented in large cells of typically 120cm x 80cm x 20cm that are equipped with two WOMs each. Here, we report on the performance of a full-scale 2x2-cell prototype detector which was exposed to 5GeV muons at the CERN PS T9 test beam facility to study the detector response and its time and spatial resolution for minimum ionising particles crossing multiple detector cells.

</details>

### [91] [Learning Population-Level Dynamics through a Latent Fokker--Planck Model and Discrepancy Transport Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21921)
*Chengyang Huang,Krishna Garikipati*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种群体级推断框架，直接从快照概率分布（snapshot probability distributions）中恢复潜在的随机动力学（latent stochastic dynamics），通过将观测演化分解为内在的Ornstein–Uhlenbeck潜过程与由单调神经网络参数化的Knothe–Rosenblatt差异传输映射（discrepancy transport map），并通过超弹性变形能（deformation energy）正则化以确保分解的唯一性与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多科学与工程系统中的观测数据是以时间为索引的概率分布，其潜在动力学未知且个体轨迹不可获取。传统的系统辨识方法依赖轨迹对应或预设演化方程，无法直接适用于此类群体级数据。

Method: 将观测到的概率演化分解为一个内在的潜随机过程（Ornstein–Uhlenbeck过程，具有Fokker–Planck方程的闭式解）与一个差异传输映射（通过Knothe–Rosenblatt重排与单调神经网络参数化）；通过基于超弹性的变形能（deformation energy）对传输映射进行正则化，以缓解潜–传输分解的非唯一性；通过统一优化问题联合学习潜随机模型与传输映射。

Result: 在涉及非线性与多模态分布动力学的数值示例中，所提框架能够准确重建复杂的概率演化，同时保持紧凑且可解析处理的潜表示。

Conclusion: 该框架为群体级动力学推断提供了一种通用方法，并为将潜随机模型与基于传输的学习推广到更一般和高维系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: Many scientific and engineering systems are observed as time-indexed probability distributions whose governing dynamics are unknown and whose individual trajectories are unavailable. These settings challenge conventional system-identification approaches that rely on trajectory correspondence or prescribed evolution equations. This work presents a population-level inference framework that recovers latent stochastic dynamics directly from snapshot probability distributions by decomposing the observed evolution into an intrinsic latent stochastic process and a discrepancy transport map that captures geometric deformation between the latent and observed probability spaces. The latent dynamics are modeled using an Ornstein--Uhlenbeck process, providing a closed-form solution to the associated Fokker--Planck equation, while the discrepancy transport map is parameterized through the Knothe--Rosenblatt rearrangement with monotone neural networks. To mitigate the non-uniqueness inherent in the latent--transport decomposition, the transport map is regularized using a deformation energy motivated by hyperelasticity, promoting smooth, physically interpretable deformations while reducing unnecessary complexity. The latent stochastic model and discrepancy transport map are learned jointly through a unified optimization problem defined over probability distributions. Numerical examples involving nonlinear and multimodal distributional dynamics demonstrate that the proposed framework accurately reconstructs complex probability evolution while preserving a compact and analytically tractable latent representation. The proposed formulation provides a general framework for population-level dynamical inference and establishes a foundation for extending latent stochastic models and transport-based learning to more general and higher-dimensional systems.

</details>

### [92] [The two momenta of an elastic rod: a Hamiltonian picture on framed Lie groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21813)
*Thomas Lessinnes*

Main category: math.DS

TL;DR: 本文通过引入数学物理中的左不变向量场（left-invariant vector fields）和Cartan–Lepage–Krupka变分形式理论，重新构建了弹性杆（elastic rods）的哈密顿框架。在该框架中，广义动量不是先验假设的，而是由变分形式自然导出的。对于孤立Cosserat杆，广义动量与材料力/力矩一致；对于存在相互作用的情况，动量的结构由内应力与相互作用贡献的叠加给出，并在肌腱驱动杆中得到了显式应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的弹性杆平衡方程可通过力/力矩平衡或势能变分得到，但复杂细丝通常更适合能量路径。然而，经典哈密顿图像要求先验地假设广义动量分量，且其物理意义随坐标选取而改变。本文旨在克服这一缺陷，建立一种动量由理论结构自然导出的哈密顿框架。

Method: 本文借鉴了两项数学物理工具：一是利用配置李群（configuration Lie group）的扩展切丛（extended tangent bundle），由左不变向量场（left-invariant vector fields）构建框架；二是采用Cartan–Lepage–Krupka变分形式理论的一维解读，在该设定下动量由变分形式强制产生，而非人为假设。Legendre变换退化为线性框架变换，扩展切丛上随之产生泊松结构（Poisson structure）。

Result: 对于孤立Cosserat杆，广义动量在任何旋转群参数化下均与材料力和力矩一致；当存在仅依赖于构型（configuration）的相互作用（如重力）时，该对应关系保持不变；而当相互作用依赖于应变（strains）时，共轭动量与内应力发生分离。由于能量具有可加性，动量分解为内应力与相互作用贡献两部分。将泊松双向量（Poisson bivector）的两个因子置于不同框架中（一个适应内应力，另一个适应共轭动量），可直接在内变量中揭示哈密顿流（Hamiltonian flow）。应用于肌腱驱动杆时，该构造避免了标准哈密顿化所需的非线性反演，将反演简化为秩一修正（rank-one correction），并直接给出显式平衡方程。

Conclusion: 本文提出的哈密顿框架无需先验假设动量形式，而是通过变分原理自然导出，统一了不同坐标系下的物理诠释。该方法在肌腱驱动杆等复杂弹性体系统中，显著简化了从拉格朗日到哈密顿形式的转换过程，具有重要的理论意义和工程应用潜力。

Abstract: The equilibrium equations of elastic rods can be obtained by balancing forces and moments, or by rendering a potential energy stationary. For complex filaments the energy route asks less of one's mechanical intuition. However, in the classical Hamiltonian picture, the components of the generalized momenta are postulated a priori and their physical meaning changes at the whim of the coordinate chart. Here, we draw on two ideas from mathematical physics. First, the extended tangent bundle of the configuration Lie group is framed by left-invariant vector fields. Second, we follow a one-dimensional reading of the Cartan--Lepage--Krupka theory of variational forms: in this setting the momenta are not postulated but forced. The Legendre transform becomes a linear change of frame, and a Poisson structure on the extended tangent bundle follows.
  For an isolated Cosserat rod, the momenta coincide, in every encoding of the rotation group, with the material force and moment familiar from rod theories. In the presence of interactions, two cases arise: interactions that depend only on the configuration, such as gravity, leave this identification intact; interactions that depend on the strains destroy it --- the conjugate momenta and the internal stresses part company. Because energies add, the momenta decompose into the internal stresses and an interaction contribution. Expressing the two factors of the Poisson bivector in different frames --- one adapted to the internal stresses, the other to the conjugate momenta --- then exposes the Hamiltonian flow directly in the internal variables.
  Applied to a tendon-actuated rod, where the standard passage to the Hamiltonian picture demands a nonlinear inversion, the construction delivers explicit equilibrium equations, the required inversion collapsing to a rank-one correction.

</details>

### [93] [An Introduction to Bayesian and Frequentist Simulation-Based Inference with Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21702)
*Maximilian Dax,Theo Heimel,Gilles Louppe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了基于机器学习的模拟推断（simulation-based inference, SBI）方法在科学和工程逆问题中的应用，涵盖了贝叶斯（Bayesian）和频率学派（frequentist）统计框架，重点介绍了神经后验估计（neural posterior estimation）和神经似然估计（neural likelihood estimation）等方法，并探讨了其在经验贝叶斯（Empirical Bayes）和解褶（unfolding）任务中的适用性，以及推断结果的验证与局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学和工程中广泛存在逆问题（inverse problems），如参数推断和探测器效应反演，传统方法在处理复杂模型时面临挑战。基于机器学习的模拟推断（SBI）提供了一种新的解决途径，因此需要系统梳理其方法论与适用边界。

Method: 以贝叶斯（Bayesian）和频率学派（frequentist）统计框架为基础，利用机器学习方法构建神经后验估计（neural posterior estimation）和神经似然估计（neural likelihood estimation），实现参数估计；同时将相同方法推广至经验贝叶斯（Empirical Bayes）和解褶（unfolding）任务。

Result: 基于机器学习的SBI方法（如神经后验估计和神经似然估计）可同时适用于贝叶斯与频率学派的参数推断，并能拓展至经验贝叶斯和解褶任务。论文还总结了推断结果的验证策略及方法本身的局限性。

Conclusion: 基于机器学习的模拟推断（SBI）是解决逆问题的有效工具，但使用时需注意其统计框架选择、结果验证方法以及内在局限。

Abstract: Simulation-based inference (SBI) with machine learning is an increasingly important tool for solving inverse problems in science and engineering, including parameter inference and the inversion of detector effects. We provide an overview of the Bayesian and frequentist statistical frameworks, describe how machine-learning-based SBI methods, such as neural posterior estimation and neural likelihood estimation, can be used for parameter estimation within these frameworks, and show that the same methods can also be applied to Empirical Bayes or unfolding tasks. We also discuss how to validate inference results and the limitations of SBI with machine learning.

</details>

### [94] [Generative and multimodal AI for materials prediction and design: Progress, challenges, and perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21660)
*Xianyuan Liu,Charles Anjah,Benjamin E. Jolly,Jonathon F. S. Markanday,Joshua Berry,Haolin Wang,Nicola A. Morley,Robert D. J. Oliver,Alexandra J. Ramadan,Delvin Ce Zhang,Katerina A. Christofidou,Haiping Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种材料性质层级体系，用于区分结构、物理和部署三类新颖性，并指出现有多模态材料数据集中在成分与理想化结构，难以支撑物理新颖性与部署新颖性；呼吁建立社区标准以支持可实现的新材料发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能（AI）在加速材料预测与设计中展现出巨大潜力，但新材料发现中的新颖性（novelty）涵盖化学合理性、结构独特性、性质相关性与实验可实现性，当前AI驱动的新颖性主张难以被充分验证和证实。

Method: 提出一种材料性质层级体系（materials property hierarchy），从内禀的、由成分决定的性质到外禀的、依赖加工过程的性能，用以澄清部署约束，并区分结构新颖性（structural novelty）、物理新颖性（physical novelty）与部署新颖性（deployment novelty）。

Result: 基于该框架审视多模态材料数据发现，现有证据集中于成分与理想化结构，而异质性、代表性不足且弱整合的数据模态限制了物理新颖性与部署新颖性的验证；当前主要依赖计算标签和代理新颖性标准（proxy novelty criteria）的基准测试存在局限。

Conclusion: 需要建立社区范围的标准，涵盖数据收集、模态对齐（modality alignment）与证据综合（evidence synthesis），以支持多模态数据构建、过程感知多模态建模、可行性优先的生成建模（feasibility-first generative modelling）以及部署感知的基准测试（deployment-aware benchmarking），从而使生成式与多模态AI能够设计出可实验实现的、具有可靠科学与实践新颖性的材料。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is accelerating materials prediction and design by enabling efficient exploration of chemical and structural spaces, with particular promise for novel materials discovery. However, novelty in materials discovery encompasses chemical plausibility, structural distinctiveness, property relevance and experimental realisability, making AI-driven novelty claims difficult to substantiate. We introduce a materials property hierarchy, from intrinsic, composition-determined properties to extrinsic, processing-dependent performance, to clarify deployment constraints and distinguish structural, physical and deployment novelty. This framework motivates an evidence-based view of multimodal materials data spanning chemical composition, microstructure, processing, and testing and characterisation, showing that current evidence remains concentrated in composition and idealised structure while heterogeneous, under-represented and weakly integrated modalities limit support for physical and deployment novelty. It also highlights the limitations of benchmarks based mainly on computational labels and proxy novelty criteria. Community-wide standards for data collection, modality alignment and evidence synthesis are needed to support multimodal data construction, process-aware multimodal modelling, feasibility-first generative modelling and deployment-aware benchmarking, so that generative and multimodal AI can design experimentally realisable materials with defensible scientific and practical novelty.

</details>


### [95] [Symmetry-Adapted Physical and Vibrational Properties of Ferroelectric Perovskite Oxides: Application to PbZrxTi1-xO3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21682)
*Sumit Ranjan Maity,Brajesh Tiwari*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文系统应用张量分析和群论方法，直接从晶体学点群对称性确定功能材料中允许的物理性质张量、振动模式对称性和拉曼选择定则，并应用于铁电材料PZT，揭示了对称性降低对物理性质张量独立分量数的影响以及拉曼光谱随组分变化的连续强度重分布特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶体对称性决定功能材料的宏观物理性质和晶格动力学，但系统地从晶体学点群对称性出发直接确定物理性质张量、振动模式对称性和拉曼选择定则的方法尚需完善，尤其对于具有复杂相图的铁电钙钛矿氧化物。

Method: 运用张量分析与群论方法，从晶体的点群对称性（crystallographic point-group symmetry）出发，推导允许的物理性质张量、振动模式对称性分类以及拉曼选择定则（Raman selection rules）。方法应用于原型铁电材料PbZrxTi1-xO3（PZT），并结合已报道的室温粉末拉曼光谱数据进行对称性分解。

Result: PZT相图中对称性降低导致热释电、介电和压电张量独立分量数增加，并改变了拉曼活性振动的对称性分类。基于对称性的拉曼光谱分解表明，准同型相界（morphotropic phase boundary）处的四方-三方相变表现为谱峰强度的连续重新分布，而非新拉曼模式的出现。部分模式中持续的亚峰结构揭示阳离子无序和晶格非谐性导致的局域对称性破缺。

Conclusion: 晶体学对称性分析对解释铁电钙钛矿氧化物的功能性质和振动性质具有重要意义，局域对称性破缺效应（阳离子无序和晶格非谐性）是理解其复杂光谱行为的关键因素。

Abstract: Crystal symmetry governs macroscopic physical properties and lattice dynamics in functional materials. We present a systematic application of tensor analysis and group theory to determine allowed physical-property tensors, vibrational-mode symmetries, and Raman selection rules directly from crystallographic point-group symmetry. The approach is applied to the prototypical ferroelectric PbZrxTi1-xO3 (PZT). Symmetry lowering across the PZT phase diagram increases the number of independent pyroelectric, dielectric, and piezoelectric tensor components and modifies the symmetry classification of Raman-active vibrations. These mode classifications enable symmetry-based decomposition of reported room-temperature powder Raman spectra as a function of composition (x) across the morphotropic phase boundary, revealing the tetragonal-to-rhombohedral transition as a continuous redistribution of spectral intensity rather than emergence of new Raman modes. Persistent subpeak structure in selected modes indicates local symmetry breaking due to cation disorder and lattice anharmonicity, underscoring the importance of crystallographic symmetry analysis for interpreting functional and vibrational properties of ferroelectric perovskite oxides.

</details>


### [96] [Ionic Diffusion Properties of Rare-Earth High-Entropy Oxides from a Machine-Learned Interatomic Potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21726)
*Mary Kathleen Caucci,Billy E. Yang,Saeed S. I. Almishal,Jon-Paul Maria,Susan B. Sinnott*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究利用基于CHGNet机器学习原子间势的经典分子动力学模拟，系统研究了铈基稀土高熵氧化物(Ce$_x$(YLaPrSm)$_{1-x}$O$_{2-δ}$)中的氧扩散行为，揭示了氧离子传输受可移动空位浓度与局域阳离子环境的双重调控，发现电导率随空位浓度呈非单调依赖关系，且提高Ce含量可通过构建低势垒扩散网络降低活化能并增强扩散率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀土高熵氧化物（RE-HEOs）因其化学复杂性、结构可调性和快速氧离子传输潜力，成为固态电化学应用中前景广阔的功能陶瓷材料，但其中氧扩散的微观机制尚未清晰。

Method: 采用Crystal Hamiltonian Graph Neural Network (CHGNet) 机器学习原子间势驱动的经典分子动力学模拟，针对镧系体系对标了三种CHGNet变体（包括基于r$^2$SCAN训练的微调模型）与明确包含f-价电子的密度泛函理论（DFT）数据。模拟涵盖了不同温度、Ce含量、氧空位浓度及萤石（fluorite）与方铁锰矿（bixbyite）两种结构。

Result: 氧传输受可移动空位浓度与局域阳离子环境的双重调控；在固定组成下，离子电导率随空位浓度呈非单调依赖关系，最优扩散出现在中等空位水平，高浓度下迁移率降低；增加Ce含量降低迁移活化能，通过Ce-Ce和Ce-Y边缘构建的低势垒扩散网络增强扩散率。

Conclusion: 机器学习原子间势能够解析化学复杂氧化物中的组成-结构-传输关系，并识别出通过成分调控增强RE-HEO材料氧离子电导率的活性路径。

Abstract: Rare-earth high-entropy oxides (RE-HEOs) have emerged as a promising class of functional ceramics for solid-state electrochemical applications due to their chemical complexity, structural tunability, and potential for fast oxygen-ion transport. In this work, we investigate oxygen diffusion in ceria-based RE-HEOs of the form Ce$_x$(YLaPrSm)$_{1-x}$O$_{2-δ}$ using classical molecular dynamics simulations driven by the Crystal Hamiltonian Graph Neural Network (CHGNet) machine-learned interatomic potential. To improve predictive accuracy for lanthanide-containing systems, we benchmark three CHGNet variants, including a fine-tuned r$^2$SCAN-trained model, against targeted density functional theory (DFT) data that explicitly include f-valence electrons. Simulations across temperature, Ce content, oxygen vacancy concentration, and both fluorite and bixbyite structures reveal that oxygen transport in RE-HEOs is governed by the interplay of two factors: the concentration of mobile vacancies and the local cation environment through which they hop. At fixed composition, ionic conductivity exhibits a non-monotonic dependence on vacancy concentration, with optimal diffusion occurring at moderate vacancy levels and reduced mobility at higher concentrations. Increasing Ce content lowers migration activation energies and enhances diffusivity through low-barrier diffusion networks built from Ce-Ce and Ce-Y edges. Analysis of individual oxygen hopping events provides atomistic insight into how local chemical environments and short-range cation ordering govern transport in high-entropy oxides. Overall, this work demonstrates that machine-learned interatomic potentials can resolve composition-structure-transport relationships in chemically complex oxides, and identifies active pathways through which compositional tuning can enhance oxygen-ion conductivity in RE-HEO materials.

</details>


### [97] [Property-Guided Diffusion for Inverse Design of Crystalline Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21849)
*Sourav Mal,Subhankar Mishra,Prasenjit Sen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于轻量级扩散模型 DiffCrysGen 的属性引导框架，通过参数高效适配器微调（parameter-efficient adapter fine-tuning）和无分类器引导（classifier-free guidance, CFG）实现高效的多属性晶体材料逆设计。系统研究了 CFG 强度对生成属性分布、晶体对称性和物理可行性的影响，在磁性和机械材料逆设计任务中分别取得了 12.3% 和 3.9% 的整体成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的属性引导晶体生成方法中，属性引导的有效性、其对晶体学对称性（crystallographic symmetry）的影响以及生成材料的物理可行性（physical viability）尚缺乏系统理解。本文旨在解决上述三个关键问题。

Method: 基于轻量级扩散模型 DiffCrysGen，采用参数高效适配器微调（parameter-efficient adapter fine-tuning）和无分类器引导（classifier-free guidance, CFG）构建属性引导框架，实现多属性晶体生成并保留无条件预训练知识。以形成能（formation energy）与饱和磁化强度（saturation magnetization）和维氏硬度（Vickers hardness）的组合作为代表性逆设计任务，系统考察不同引导强度下的生成效果。生成结构先进行几何预筛选，再通过基于机器学习原子间势（machine-learning interatomic potential, MLIP）的工作流进行结构弛豫、热力学、动力学及属性特异性验证。

Result: 增大 CFG 引导尺度（guidance scale）能够使生成结构的属性分布逐渐逼近目标值，同时降低最低对称性（$P1$）结构的比例并增加高对称性结构占比。该框架在热力学和动力学稳定的磁性材料及机械硬质材料的逆设计任务中，分别取得了 12.3% 和 3.9% 的整体成功率。

Conclusion: 属性引导的 DiffCrysGen 被确立为一种高效的逆材料设计（inverse materials design）框架，同时为无分类器引导在晶体生成中的作用提供了新的见解。

Abstract: Diffusion-based generative models with property guidance have emerged as a promising paradigm for inverse materials design by enabling the generation of crystalline materials with user-specified target properties. However, despite recent advances, the effectiveness of property guidance, its influence on crystallographic symmetry, and the physical viability of generated materials remain poorly understood. To address these questions, we develop a property-guided framework based on the lightweight diffusion model DiffCrysGen using parameter-efficient adapter fine-tuning and classifier-free guidance (CFG). The resulting framework enables efficient multi-property crystal generation while preserving the knowledge learned during unconditional pre-training. Using formation energy together with saturation magnetization and Vickers hardness as representative inverse-design tasks, we systematically investigate the influence of CFG across a broad range of guidance strengths. Increasing the guidance scale progressively steers the generated property distributions toward the prescribed targets while reducing the fraction of lowest-symmetry ($P1$) structures and increasing the proportion of higher-symmetry structures. To evaluate physical viability, generated structures are geometrically prescreened and subsequently validated using a machine-learning interatomic potential (MLIP)-based workflow comprising structural relaxation and thermodynamic, dynamical, and property-specific analyses. The framework identifies thermodynamically and dynamically stable magnetic and mechanically hard materials with overall success rates of 12.3\% and 3.9\%, respectively. These results establish property-guided DiffCrysGen as an efficient framework for inverse materials design while providing new insights into the role of classifier-free guidance in crystal generation.

</details>


### [98] [Effect of Al-Zn alloy wafer grain boundary diffusion on the magnetism and microstructure of sintered NdFeB magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21870)
*Xi Liu,Wenxi Fang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究使用 Al₈₀Zn₂₀ 合金片对烧结 Nd-Fe-B 磁体进行晶界扩散（grain boundary diffusion, GBD）处理，系统研究了处理温度（700°C 和 900°C）对磁性能和微观结构的影响。900°C 处理使矫顽力（coercivity）从 951.5 kA/m 提升至 1158.2 kA/m（增幅 21.7%），归因于晶界相优化、核壳结构形成及晶粒边缘平滑化三种协同机制，提供了一条非重稀土（non-heavy-rare-earth）的矫顽力增强路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 烧结 Nd-Fe-B 磁体广泛应用于高温领域，但其矫顽力（coercivity）通常依赖重稀土（heavy rare earth）元素添加，成本高且资源受限。探索非重稀土路径来提升矫顽力具有重要的工程应用价值。本研究正是基于此背景，系统评估 Al-Zn 晶界扩散（grain boundary diffusion, GBD）处理作为替代方案的可行性。

Method: 以 Al₈₀Zn₂₀ 合金片为扩散源，置于圆柱形烧结 Nd-Fe-B 样品两端，分别在 900°C 和 700°C 下真空（≤5×10⁻³ Pa）扩散退火 7 小时，随后在 500°C 回火 2 小时。采用磁性能测量、扫描电子显微镜（SEM）、能量色散 X 射线光谱（EDS）和 X 射线衍射仪（XRD）对样品进行表征分析。

Result: 矫顽力在 900°C 处理后从 951.5 kA/m 提升至 1158.2 kA/m（增幅 206.7 kA/m，21.7%），700°C 处理后提升至 1039.6 kA/m（增幅 88.1 kA/m，9.3%）。900°C 处理后剩磁（remanence）从 1282 mT 小幅下降至 1256 mT。900°C 处理形成了更薄、更连续的晶界相（grain boundary phase）及主相晶粒周围的核壳结构（core-shell structure）。EDS 显示 Al 优先富集于 Nd₂Fe₁₄B 晶粒的壳层，Zn 主要分布于晶界相中降低其熔点并改善流动性。XRD 未检测到二次相，但观察到轻微晶格膨胀，表明部分 Al 取代了主相中的 Fe。

Conclusion: Al-Zn 晶界扩散（GBD）处理可有效提升烧结 Nd-Fe-B 磁体的矫顽力（coercivity），900°C 的处理效果显著优于 700°C。矫顽力增强归因于三种协同机制：晶界去耦（grain boundary decoupling）改善、高各向异性壳层（high-anisotropy shell layer）增强畴壁钉扎（domain-wall pinning），以及晶粒边缘光滑化抑制反磁畴成核（reverse-domain nucleation）。该方法提供了一条非重稀土（non-heavy-rare-earth）途径，适用于高温应用场景。

Abstract: This study systematically investigates Al-Zn grain boundary diffusion (GBD) treatment on sintered Nd-Fe-B magnets using $Al_{80}Zn_{20}$ alloy sheets as the diffusion source. The alloy sheets were placed at both ends of cylindrical samples and diffusion-annealed at 900$^\circ$C and 700$^\circ$C for 7 hours under vacuum ($\leq5\times10^{-3}$ Pa), followed by tempering at 500$^\circ$C for 2 hours. Magnetic measurements show that coercivity increases from 951.5kA/m in the untreated sample to 1158.2kA/m at 900$^\circ$C (a gain of 206.7kA/m, 21.7\%) and to 1039.6kA/m at 700$^\circ$C (a gain of 88.1kA/m, 9.3\%), while remanence declines modestly from 1282mT to 1256mT after the high-temperature treatment. Scanning electron microscopy (SEM), energy-dispersive X-ray spectroscopy (EDS), and X-ray diffractometer (XRD) analyses reveal that the 900$^\circ$C treatment produces a thinner, more continuous grain boundary phase and a distinct core-shell structure around the main-phase grains. EDS mapping shows that Al preferentially enriches the shell region of the $Nd_2Fe_{14}B$ grains, while Zn predominantly resides in the grain boundary phase, where it lowers the melting point of the intergranular phase and improves its fluidity. XRD confirms that no secondary phases are formed, though a slight lattice expansion suggests partial Al substitution for Fe in the main phase. Verified by computational analysis, the coercivity enhancement is attributed to three synergistic factors: improved grain boundary decoupling, the formation of a high-anisotropy shell layer that strengthens domain-wall pinning, and the smoothing of grain edges to suppress reverse-domain nucleation. Overall, the 900$\circ$C treatment proves considerably more effective than 700$\circ$C, providing a non-heavy-rare-earth pathway for enhancing coercivity in sintered Nd-Fe-B magnets for high-temperature applications.

</details>


### [99] [Crystallization of pristine cubic ice from liquid at ambient pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21872)
*Chenwei Zheng,Paul F. Henry,Aasim I. Shaffi,Sanghamitra Mukhopadhyay,Miroslava Novoveska,Milz L. Beaumont,Yidan Wang,Camilla Di Mino,Tom F. Headen,Marta Falkowska,Christopher A. Howard,Adam J. Clancy,Christoph G. Salzmann,Neal T. Skipper*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过将水溶液限制在介孔二氧化硅中冷却，能够在常压下直接生成纯净的立方冰Ic，且该相是唯一热力学稳定的结晶相，表明其在自然与合成过程中具有此前未被充分认识的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冰的相图十分丰富，已识别出超过二十种结晶多晶型（polymorph）。其中，低压“冰I”族中的六方冰Ih（hexagonal ice Ih）是地球上的主要形式，而立方冰Ic（cubic ice Ic）则难以获得。关于立方冰Ic能否直接从液态形成、其热力学稳定性及自然存在等基本问题仍悬而未决。

Method: 在常压条件下，将水溶液限制在介孔二氧化硅（mesoporous silica）中并冷却，以制备立方冰Ic。主要利用中子散射（neutron scattering）技术对产物进行表征和确认。

Result: 在该受限条件下，纯净的立方冰Ic可重复地直接生成，并且是唯一热力学稳定的结晶水相。该结果明确表明，立方冰Ic可以直接从液态获得，并在常压下保持稳定。

Conclusion: 立方冰Ic在自然和合成过程中所扮演的角色比以往认为的更为重要。这一发现对理解冰的多晶型行为及其在地球科学、材料科学等领域的意义具有启示作用。

Abstract: The phase diagram of frozen water is famously rich: to date, over twenty crystalline polymorphs have been identified. Of the low-pressure 'ice I' family, hexagonal (Ih) is the principal form on Earth, while cubic (Ic) is much more elusive. Fundamental questions remain open as to whether cubic ice Ic can form directly from the liquid state, its thermodynamic stability and natural occurrence. Here we show that pristine cubic ice Ic can be formed at atmospheric pressure simply by cooling an aqueous solution confined within mesoporous silica. Using primarily neutron scattering, we show unambiguously that under these conditions, cubic ice Ic forms reproducibly and is the only thermodynamically stable crystalline phase of water. The discovery that cubic ice Ic is directly accessible from the liquid state, and stable at atmospheric pressure, strongly suggests that this polymorph plays a much more significant role in natural and synthetic processes than previously thought.

</details>


### [100] [Switchable Altermagnetism via Spin-Induced Improper Polarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21930)
*Zhihao Dai,Michele Reticcioli,Wei Ren,Hongjun Xiang,Alessandro Stroppa*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种在非极性双亚晶格磁体中通过自旋诱导的非本征极化（spin-induced improper polarization）实现可逆自旋分裂切换的新方法，并在DyFeO3中通过密度泛函理论（DFT）验证了该机制，为可切换的交变磁体（altermagnet）在非极性体材料中的应用开辟了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无杂散场的交变磁体（altermagnet）中实现可逆的自旋分裂切换（reversible spin-splitting switching）对自旋电子学（spintronics）应用具有重要意义，但此前仅限于极少数极性材料，亟需更普适的实现方案。

Method: 提出基于自旋诱导非本征极化（spin-induced improper polarization）的新机制，适用于非极性双亚晶格磁体（nonpolar dual-sublattice magnets）。以DyFeO3为例，利用密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）进行计算验证。

Result: 在DyFeO3中，非极性的Fe和Dy自旋模式的乘积可转化为诱导的极化模式。DFT计算表明：Dy与Fe之间的相对自旋排列决定极化方向，而Fe亚晶格（sublattice）控制非相对论性自旋分裂（nonrelativistic spin splitting），从而实现了可逆切换。

Conclusion: 该研究证明自旋诱导的非本征极化是实现在非极性体材料中可切换交变磁体（switchable altermagnetism）的一条有效路径。

Abstract: Enabling reversible spin-splitting switching in stray-field-free altermagnets is promising for spintronic applications, but currently limited to a narrow class of polar materials. We propose a broader approach based on spin-induced improper polarization in nonpolar dual-sublattice magnets. We demonstrate this mechanism in DyFeO3, where the product of nonpolar Fe and Dy spin modes transforms as an induced polar mode. Density functional theory shows that the relative Dy--Fe spin alignment selects the polarization, while the Fe sublattice controls nonrelativistic spin splitting, thus enabling reversible switching. These results establish spin-induced improper polarization as a route to switchable altermagnetism in nonpolar bulk systems.

</details>


### [101] [Accelerating grain boundary modelling and simulation by automated pre-evaluation of the irreducible macroscopic representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22090)
*Wei Wan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出一种算法，用于确定立方晶系中重合位点阵（coincident-site-lattice, CSL）晶界（grain boundary, GB）的不可约宏观表征及其超胞的不可约尺寸，从而有效节省计算资源，并利用该尺寸参数预测值得关注的晶界特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶界（grain boundary, GB）的建模与模拟面临系统尺寸与计算能力之间的矛盾，这限制了计算可触及的晶界特征范围。因此，需要一种方法在保证表征完整性的前提下缩小超胞尺寸，以降低计算开销。

Method: 提出一种算法，用于寻找立方晶系中任意给定重合位点阵（coincident-site-lattice, CSL）晶界特征的不可约宏观表征，并确定其超胞的不可约尺寸。该算法与传统的正交超胞方法及已发表的另一种计算方法进行了对比评估。

Result: 该算法能够确定超胞的不可约尺寸参数，可用于预测5维空间中相对特殊的晶界特征—具有小结构单元（structural units）的晶界更可能表现出值得关注的结构-性能关系。这一预测通过铝混合晶界的能量和迁移率趋势的原子模拟得到了验证。

Conclusion: 所提出的算法在节省计算资源方面具有优势，并且超胞尺寸参数可作为筛选具有特殊结构-性能关系的晶界的有效预判指标。

Abstract: A major challenge in the modelling and simulation of grain boundaries (GBs) is the conflict between the system size and computation capability, which inherently restricts the computationally accessible boundary characters. We present an algorithm to find the irreducible macroscopic representation of any given coincident-site-lattice GB character in the cubic lattice, and thus determine the irreducible size of its supercell. The algorithm is compared with the conventional orthogonal supercell and a published calculation method to assess its merits in saving computational resources. This supercell size parameter can be used to predict which GB character is relatively special across the vast 5D space, as those GBs possess small structural units are likely to exhibit particular structure-property relationships that are worthy of attention. The prediction is confirmed by examining the energy and mobility trends of aluminum mixed GBs obtained from the atomistic simulations.

</details>


### [102] [Mechanical mapping of thin elastic films and living cells with spherical tip atomic force microscopy probes at large indentations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22167)
*Gabriel Gomila,Mauricio Cano,Beatriz Cantero,Sophie Strawbridge,Lara Aiassa,Loris Rizzello,Giuseppe Battaglia,Eleni Dalaka,Jordi Comelles,Annalisa Calò*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出了一种分析模型，用于量化使用球形尖端原子力显微镜（AFM）在弹性薄膜和活细胞上获得的大压痕力曲线。该模型考虑了整个压痕范围内的基底效应，克服了Sneddon和Hertz接触模型（仅适用于半无限厚样品）以及带有基底效应修正（BEC）的抛物面尖端模型（仅适用于较小压痕）的局限性。模型在聚丙烯酰胺（PAA）水凝胶薄膜上得到了实验验证，结果吻合极佳。模型还成功应用于活巨噬细胞，给出了准确的杨氏模量值（约200 Pa），其误差显著小于传统模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AFM接触力学模型（如Sneddon和Hertz模型）仅适用于半无限厚样品，带有基底效应修正（BEC）的抛物面模型也仅适用于小压痕范围。亟需一个能够在整个压痕范围内有效处理基底效应（bottom effect）的分析模型，以准确量化弹性薄膜和活细胞等软材料的力学性质。

Method: 提出了一个分析模型，用于量化球形尖端AFM在大压痕下获得的作用力曲线。模型纳入了整个压痕范围内的基底效应，并对实验测得的聚丙烯酰胺（PAA）水凝胶薄膜及活巨噬细胞（live macrophages）的作用力曲线进行验证。

Result: 在PAA薄膜上获得了极佳的拟合结果，并发现当薄膜厚度低于临界值（约15 μm）时，本征杨氏模量（intrinsic Young's modulus）增加。对于活巨噬细胞，模型给出准确的杨氏模量值（约200 Pa）。与传统Sneddon模型（偏差100%）和带BEC的抛物面模型（偏差-25%）相比，所提模型的杨氏模量提取值显著不同。

Conclusion: 该模型能够在大压痕条件下利用球形尖端准确分析弹性薄膜材料和活细胞的力曲线测量结果，在微纳米尺度力学表征中具有重要应用潜力。

Abstract: An analytical model to quantify large indentation force curves acquired on elastic thin films and living cells with spherical tip Atomic Force Microscopy (AFM) probes is presented. The model accounts for the bottom effect in the whole indentation range and overcomes the limitations of Sneddon's and Hertz's contact models, which are valid for semi-infinite thick samples, and of paraboloid tip models with bottom effect correction (BEC) that are applicable to spherical tips only for relatively small indentations. The model is experimentally validated with force volume measurements on polyacrylamide (PAA) hydrogel thin films, where an excellent agreement is obtained. The accurate correction of the bottom effect demonstrates that the intrinsic Young's modulus of PAA thin films increases for thickness below a critical value (~15 um). The model also shows excellent agreement with force curves acquired on live macrophages, providing accurate Young's modulus values for these very soft cells (E~200 Pa). Young's modulus values extracted with the proposed model significantly differ from those obtained from Sneddon's or paraboloid models with BEC, whose values deviate by 100% and -25%, respectively. Results show the potential of the proposed model for analysing force curve measurements with spherical tips at large indentations on thin film elastic materials and living cells at the micro and nanoscale.

</details>


### [103] [From Amorphous to Amorphous-Crystalline Mixed-Phase Boron Nitride: Evolution of the Thermal and Elastic Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22185)
*Jiaqi Yang,Thomas Souvignet,Onurcan Kaya,Peng Xiao,Emigdio Chavez-Angel,Daniel Capolat Palomar,Zuzanna Ewa Kaczmarska,Bartlomiej Graczykowski,Javier Rodriguez Viejo,Clivia M. Sotomayor Torres,Catherine Marichy,Catherine Journet,Stephan Roche,Marianna Sledzinska*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了非晶氮化硼（amorphous boron nitride, aBN）薄膜的纳米尺度热输运与弹性响应，通过化学气相沉积合成不同结构薄膜，结合实验测量与分子动力学模拟揭示结构有序度对导热系数和杨氏模量的调控规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非晶氮化硼（aBN）作为有前景的介电与保护涂层材料，其纳米尺度的散热性能和弹性响应尚缺乏定量表征，限制了其在纳米电子学、互连与涂层中的应用优化。

Method: 采用硼嗪（borazine）基化学气相沉积（chemical vapor deposition, CVD）在800–1000 °C合成BN薄膜；使用频域热反射法（frequency-domain thermoreflectance）测量热导率；利用微布里渊光散射（micro-Brillouin light scattering）与有限元建模（finite-element modelling）获取弹性模量；结合Green-Kubo分子动力学（molecular dynamics）模拟解释微观机制。

Result: 完全非晶aBN薄膜的面外热导率（kout）呈超低且厚度依赖性，10–40 nm时低于0.5 W m⁻¹ K⁻¹；随结晶度增加，热导率升至1.5 W m⁻¹ K⁻¹；杨氏模量从800 °C时的7.5 ± 0.7 GPa上升至1000 °C时的53 ± 5 GPa，伴随结构硬化。

Conclusion: 键合拓扑（bonding topology）与振动输运（vibrational transport）主导热-力学性能变化；氧、氢、碳杂质及成分可作为实际调控手段，进一步优化BN薄膜的热与力学响应，以改善纳米电子学、互连与涂层应用。

Abstract: Amorphous boron nitride (aBN) is a promising dielectric and protective coating, yet its nanoscale heat dissipation and elastic response remain poorly quantified. Here we synthesize a variety of BN thin films by borazine-based chemical vapor deposition (800-1000 C) and study the temperature-driven structural transition from fully amorphous networks to mixed amorphous-crystalline films with embedded BN nanocrystallites.Frequency-domain thermoreflectance data show an ultralow, thickness-dependent cross-plane thermal conductivity for aBN (kout < 0.5 W m-1 K-1 for 10-40 nm), which increases systematically with crystalline order up to 1.5 W m-1 K-1. Micro-Brillouin light scattering and finite-element modelling reveal a concomitant stiffening, with Young's modulus rising from 7.5 +/- 0.7 GPa (800 C) to 53 +/- 5 GPa (1000 C). Green-Kubo molecular dynamics simulations rationalize these trends via bonding topology and vibrational transport, and highlight how oxygen, hydrogen and carbon impurities and composition provide practical knobs to further tune the thermal and mechanical responses in BN films for improving nano-electronics, interconnects and coating applications.

</details>


### [104] [Substrate-metal interface engineering enhances TaN/Ta thin film superconducting resonator performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22294)
*Moritz Singer,Harsh Gupta,Benedikt Schoof,Elena Willinger,Anton Orekhov,Alexandra Schewski,Samuil Kostadinov,Philip Constantin Schneider,Marc Tornow*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究系统探究了氮化钽（tantalum nitride）薄膜及其与钽（tantalum）薄膜组合在硅衬底上的超导谐振器性能，发现引入薄层Ta缓冲层可显著提升内部品质因子（internal quality factor, Qi）至5.9×10^5，机制在于抑制TaN-Si界面的氮积累与结构无序，从而降低二能级系统（two-level system）损耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钽（tantalum）已被证明是超导量子比特（superconducting qubits）的理想材料，但其氮化物（nitrides）受到的关注相对较少。氮化钽（tantalum nitride）因具有多种化学计量比（stoichiometries），可展现出超导相与非超导相，能在超导量子比特中发挥多重功能，但其薄膜性能尚缺乏系统研究。

Method: 研究在硅衬底上制备了超导TaN与Ta薄膜组合，测量其在单光子区间（single-photon regime）、100mK温度下的内部品质因子（internal quality factor, Qi），并结合透射电子显微镜（transmission electron microscopy, TEM）对界面微观结构进行表征。

Result: 单独TaN薄膜的Qi约为1.5×10^5；在数纳米厚TaN籽晶层（seed layer）上生长的Ta谐振器性能与之相当。而在Si衬底与TaN籽晶层之间引入数纳米厚的Ta缓冲层（buffer layer）后，Qi显著提升至5.9×10^5。TEM显示TaN-Si界面存在氮积累与结构无序，Ta缓冲层可抑制此界面修饰效应。

Conclusion: 谐振器性能的提升与界面相关二能级系统（two-level system）损耗的减少一致，有力地支持了控制衬底-金属界面（substrate-metal interface）对于超导量子比特电路性能至关重要的假设。

Abstract: Tantalum has been demonstrated as a promising material for superconducting qubits. However, comparatively little attention has been given to its nitrides. Tantalum nitride exhibits a range of stoichiometries, resulting in a variety of material properties, including both superconducting and non-superconducting phases. Owing to this versatility, tantalum nitrides can serve multiple purposes in superconducting qubits: as seed layers for alpha-Ta growth, as a superconducting base material and as a non-superconducting barrier in the Josephson junction. In this study, we explore the performance of superconducting TaN and Ta thin film combinations on silicon substrates in terms of internal quality factor Qi. We find that standalone TaN films exhibit Qi values of about 1.5x10^5 at 100mK in the single-photon regime. Surprisingly, a resonator made from Ta grown on a few-nanometers-thick TaN seed layer yields largely the same performance. However, adding an additional, few-nanometers-thick Ta buffer layer between the Si substrate and this TaN seed layer enhances Qi significantly up to 5.9x10^5. Supporting transmission electron microscopy measurements reveal nitrogen accumulation and structural disorder at the TaN-Si interface, while this interfacial modification is suppressed when the Ta buffer layer is introduced. The observed improvement in resonator performance is consistent with a reduction of interface-related two-level system losses and strongly supports the hypothesis that controlling the substrate-metal interface is pivotal for the performance of superconducting qubit circuitry.

</details>


### [105] [Machine Learning Inference Limits of Routine Cement Characterization for CEM I Performance: Evidence From a Multi-Producer Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22512)
*Marchellino Ghorayeb,Christiane Rößler,Horst-Michael Ludwig,Leon Herrmann,Stefan Kollmannsberger*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 基于23家欧洲生产商476份水泥记录，结合机器学习归因与生产商留出测试，分析了常规水泥表征数据（CEM I）对强度等级、用水量等性能指标的推断能力及其跨生产商可迁移性。研究表明，细度是最强的单一描述子，但氧化物化学贡献了可比信号；强度等级与用水量可被有效恢复，但早期强度仅作为群体趋势而非可分离物理解；留出测试表明绝对预测误差在不同生产商间相当，但生产商内强度变异的恢复程度因厂而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规水泥性能表征（cement performance characterization）可提供持续的质量控制数据，但其在性能推断（performance inference）以及跨独立生产商的可迁移性（transferability）方面的信息含量仍不确定。本研究旨在量化这些常规测量数据对水泥性能的推断能力。

Method: 分析了来自23家欧洲生产商的476份水泥记录，这些记录在27年间于同一实验室收集。研究聚焦CEM I水泥，结合了氧化物化学、勃氏细度（Blaine fineness）、粒度分布描述子（particle-size distribution descriptors）、物理性质以及衍生Bogue描述子和当量碱描述子，采用机器学习归因（machine learning attribution）与生产商留出测试（producer-transfer tests）进行分析。

Result: 对于CEM I，细度是强度等级和用水量最强的描述子族，但氧化物化学在联合评估时贡献了可比信号。勃氏细度与紧凑粒度分布在描述子空间内基本可互换，表明常规测量已捕获了可恢复的主要细度信息。当量碱（equivalent alkali）与28天强度呈一致负相关，在该数据集中通过K₂O体现。强度等级和用水量可从常规表征数据中恢复；早期强度仅作为群体水平趋势（population-level tendency）恢复，而非物理可分离的类别。生产商留出测试显示绝对预测误差在不同留出生产商间相当，但生产商内强度变异的恢复程度具有生产商依赖性。

Conclusion: 常规CEM I表征数据支持跨生产商的有用性能推断，同时揭示了生产商特异性变异（producer-specific variation），后者的恢复可能需要额外的物相形态分析（additional speciation）。

Abstract: Routine cement performance characterization provides continuous quality control data, but its information content for performance inference and transferability across independent producers remains uncertain. This study analyzes 476 cement records from 23 European producers, collected in one laboratory over 27 years, to determine what can be inferred from routine measurements. The analysis focuses on CEM I and combines oxide chemistry, Blaine fineness, particle-size distribution descriptors, physical properties, and derived Bogue and equivalent-alkali descriptors with machine learning attribution and producer-transfer tests.
  For CEM I, fineness is the strongest descriptor family for strength class and water demand, but oxide chemistry contributes a comparable signal when evaluated jointly. Blaine and compact particle-size distribution representations are largely interchangeable within the descriptor space, indicating that the dominant recoverable fineness information is captured by routine measurements. Equivalent alkali shows a consistent negative association with 28-day strength, through K$_2$O in this dataset.
  Strength class and water demand can be recovered from routine cement characterization data. The early-strength designation is recovered only as a population-level tendency, not a physically separable class, because early-strength development can arise from combinations of fineness, sulfate--alkali chemistry, phase assemblage, and plant practice. Producer-holdout tests show that absolute prediction errors remain comparable across the held-out producers in this dataset, whereas recovery of within-producer strength variation is producer-dependent. Routine CEM I characterization therefore supports useful performance inference across producers, while exposing producer-specific variation whose recovery may require additional speciation.

</details>

### [106] [Cross-section measurements of boosted Higgs boson production in final states with pairs of hadronically decaying $τ$-leptons with the ATLAS experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22193)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文基于ATLAS实验的Run 2（140 fb⁻¹，13 TeV）和部分Run 3（162 fb⁻¹，13.6 TeV）数据，测量了高洛伦兹 boosted Higgs 玻色子在双强子衰变τ轻子末态中的产生截面，在 Higgs 玻色子横动量高于300 GeV的 boosted 区域，观测（预期）显著性为3.8（3.3）个标准差，提供了该区域双τ末态 Higgs 产生的实验证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前 $H \to \tau\tau$ 测量在 Higgs 玻色子横动量（transverse momentum）高于300 GeV的 boosted 区域灵敏度有限，标准强子τ轻子重建效率在该相空间（phase space）内不足，因此需要专门的 boost 双τ重建与鉴别技术来扩展测量范围。

Method: 采用专用的大半径喷注（large radius jet）中对分离距离较小的τ轻子对进行重建，结合专用的 boosted di-τ 重建与辨识（identification）技术，并利用多变量分析方法（multivariate analysis methods）提取信号。数据来源为ATLAS实验在质心能量 $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV（积分亮度140 fb⁻¹的完整Run 2样本）和 $\sqrt{s}=13.6$ TeV（积分亮度162 fb⁻¹的部分Run 3样本）下的质子-质子对撞数据。

Result: 在 Higgs 玻色子横动量高于300 GeV的区域内，给出了胶子-胶子融合（gluon-gluon fusion）产生过程的微分截面测量（基于简化模板截面框架），以及矢量玻色子融合（vector-boson fusion）产生过程的 inclusive 截面测量。观测（预期）显著性为3.8（3.3）个标准差。

Conclusion: 该测量为 Higgs 玻色子在 boosted 双τ末态中的产生提供了实验证据（evidence），并进一步拓展了高横动量区域 Higgs 产生截面的实验知识。

Abstract: Cross section measurements of high-Lorentz boosted Higgs boson production in final states with pairs of hadronically decaying $τ$-leptons are presented. The analysis is based on the full Run 2 data sample of proton-proton collisions at a centre-of-mass energy of $\sqrt{s} = 13$ TeV, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 140 $\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$, and a partial Run 3 data sample at $\sqrt{s} = 13.6$ TeV with an integrated luminosity of 162 $\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$ collected in 2022-2024 with the ATLAS experiment. The analysis extends the sensitivity of previous $H \to ττ$ measurements to Higgs boson transverse momenta above 300 GeV by reconstructing $τ$-lepton pairs produced with a small separation in large radius jets. The standard reconstruction of hadronic $τ$-leptons has limited efficiency in this phase space, requiring dedicated boosted di-$τ$ reconstruction and identification techniques, combined with multivariate analysis methods, to extract the signal. For Higgs boson transverse momenta above 300 GeV, results are presented for both inclusive and differential cross-sections. Within the simplified template cross-section framework, differential cross-section measurements in Higgs boson transverse momentum for gluon-gluon fusion production are provided, together with an inclusive cross-section measurement for vector-boson fusion production. For the inclusive measurement, the analysis yields an observed (expected) significance of 3.8 (3.3) standard deviations over the background-only hypothesis, providing evidence of Higgs boson production in the boosted regime of the di-$τ$ final state.

</details>


### [107] [Decorrelation of neural networks from particle lifetimes in the LHCb topological $b$ trigger](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22281)
*Johannes Albrecht,Alessandro Bertolin,James Connaughton,Jonathan Davies,Blaise Delaney,Agnieszka Dziurda,Conor Fitzpatrick,Maciej Giza,Vava V. Gligorov,James A. Gooding,Nicole Schulte,Nicole Skidmore,Mika Vesterinen,Mike Williams,Shunan Zhang,Valeriia Zhovkovska*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文介绍了LHCb拓扑美触发器（topological beauty trigger）中用于抑制神经网络评分与b-强子寿命之间在大寿命区域相关性的两种方法，以保障后续物理分析的无偏性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LHCb的软件触发系统采用单调Lipschitz神经网络（monotonic Lipschitz neural networks）筛选含b-强子的碰撞事件，但触发选择必须在大寿命区域对b-强子寿命保持无偏。然而，在多重质子-质子对撞同时发生的繁忙探测器环境中，误关联的衰变产物可能导致重建出人工增大的寿命，从而引入偏差。

Method: 论文提出了两种方法来缓解神经网络打分与候选粒子寿命之间在大寿命区域的相关性，并对所得模型的性能进行了评估。

Result: 基于提出的两种方法训练得到的模型在减少神经网络评分与寿命之间的相关性方面取得了效果，具体定量结果摘要中未详述。

Conclusion: 两种方法均能有效降低大寿命区域中触发选择对b-强子寿命的依赖性，从而提升在繁忙探测器环境下重建的可靠性及物理分析的无偏性。

Abstract: The LHCb topological beauty trigger is the primary set of algorithms for selecting collision events containing $b$-hadrons in the fully software-based LHCb trigger. The algorithms apply monotonic Lipschitz neural networks (NNs) to select vertices of charged particles consistent with the distinct topology of a $b$ decay, i.e., those with large lifetimes and transverse momentum. Many analyses of the events recorded require that the selection must be unbiased with respect to the $b$-hadron lifetime at large lifetimes. Accurate reconstruction is challenging in busier detector environments, in which several visible proton-proton collisions occur simultaneously per bunch crossing, such that misassociation of decay products can result in vertices with artificially large measured lifetimes. This paper presents two approaches to mitigate correlations between NN scores and candidate lifetimes at large lifetime, and evaluates the performance of the resulting models.

</details>

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文基于Pierre Auger观测站的混合测量数据，发现高能强子相互作用模型普遍低估了质心能量√s=97.7 TeV下空气簇射最大深度⟨X_max⟩的观测值。通过建立次级粒子能谱变量与⟨X_max⟩之间的普适关系，将Auger数据偏好的⟨X_max⟩映射到这些变量上，表明所有模型都需要增加平均弹性（mean elasticity）和强子能量比例（fraction of hadronic energy），后者需放大2.8到4.6倍才能解释“μ子疑难”（muon puzzle）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能宇宙射线（high-energy cosmic rays）与大气相互作用的强子相互作用模型（hadronic interaction models）存在系统性偏差，尤其在高能区。实验测量与模型预测之间在空气簇射最大深度⟨X_max⟩上存在显著差异，且这一差异可能与长期未解的μ子疑难（muon puzzle）相关。

Method: 利用Pierre Auger观测站的混合测量（hybrid measurements）数据，以质心能量√s=97.7 TeV的质子诱导空气簇射（proton-induced air showers）为研究对象，分析强子相互作用模型预测的⟨X_max⟩与次级粒子（secondary particles）能谱特征变量均值之间的普适关系（universal relation），并将Auger数据偏好的⟨X_max⟩映射至这些变量。

Result: 所有被检验的强子相互作用模型均支持增加平均弹性（mean elasticity）和强子能量比例（fraction of hadronic energy）。其中，强子能量比例需放大2.8到4.6倍才能与Auger观测数据一致，从而解释μ子疑难（muon puzzle）。

Conclusion: 高能强子相互作用模型在描述宇宙射线空气簇射方面存在系统性低估，需通过调整次级粒子能谱特征——尤其是增加平均弹性和强子能量比例——来弥合理论与实验之间的差距。

Abstract: Hybrid measurements at the Pierre Auger Observatory indicate that most high-energy hadronic interaction models underestimate the average depth of the shower maximum, $\langle X_{\max} \rangle$, at a center-of-mass energy of $\sqrt{s}=97.7 \pm 0.4^{+6.6}_{-6.2}\,\mathrm{TeV}$. In this Letter, the hadronic interaction models are shown to follow a universal relation between the predicted $\langle X_{\max} \rangle$ and the mean values of variables characterizing the energy spectra of secondary particles produced in the first interaction of proton-induced air showers. Assuming the validity of these relations in Nature, we map the values of $\langle X_{\max} \rangle$ favored by Auger data into mean values of these variables. All models favor an increase in the mean elasticity and in the fraction of hadronic energy in proton--air interactions. The latter must be amplified by a factor of $2.8$ to $4.6$ to account for the muon puzzle.

</details>

Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文基于BESIII探测器在BEPCII对撞机上收集的44 fb⁻¹积分亮度的e⁺e⁻碰撞数据，首次测量了e⁺e⁻→K_S⁰\barΞ⁺Σ⁻+c.c.过程在56个质心能量点（3.510至4.951 GeV）的Born截面。通过假设幂律函数加粲偶素（charmonium）或类粲偶素共振的拟合，未观察到任何粲偶素态衰变至该末态的信号，并在90%置信水平下给出了各共振态的电子宽度与分支比乘积的上限。结合同旋对称过程的结果，发现两个过程的Born截面比约为1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QCD（量子色动力学，quantum chromodynamics）中粲偶素（charmonium）物理是一个重要研究领域，而e⁺e⁻湮灭过程的多重子产生机制尚不明确。实验上对e⁺e⁻→K_S⁰\barΞ⁺Σ⁻+c.c.过程的Born截面测量此前尚无报道，该测量有助于检验粲偶素衰变中的强相互作用机制及同位旋对称性。

Method: 利用BESIII探测器在BEPCII对撞机上采集的44 fb⁻¹积分亮度的e⁺e⁻碰撞数据，在3.510至4.951 GeV范围内56个质心能量点测量该过程的Born截面。采用幂律函数（power-law function）叠加粲偶素或类粲偶素共振（包括ψ(3770)、ψ(4040)、ψ(4160)、Y(4230)、Y(4360)、ψ(4415)、Y(4500)、Y(4660)、Y(4710)）的模型对穿衣截面（dressed cross sections）进行拟合。

Result: 未发现任何粲偶素或类粲偶素共振态显著衰变至K_S⁰\barΞ⁺Σ⁻+c.c.过程的信号。给出了各共振态在90%置信水平下的电子宽度与分支比乘积的上限。将结果与同位旋对称过程e⁺e⁻→K⁻\barΞ⁺Σ⁰+c.c.的截面测量对比，得到两个过程Born截面的比值R≈1。

Conclusion: e⁺e⁻→K_S⁰\barΞ⁺Σ⁻+c.c.过程的Born截面在误差范围内与同位旋对称过程e⁺e⁻→K⁻\barΞ⁺Σ⁰+c.c.的截面之比约为1，表明该反应过程可能满足同位旋对称性（isospin symmetry）的预期。未观察到粲偶素态衰变至该末态的显著信号，相应上限已给出。

Abstract: Using $e^+e^-$ collision data collected with the BESIII detector at the BEPCII collider corresponding to a total integrated luminosity of 44~fb$^{-1}$, we present the first measurement of the Born cross sections for the process $e^+e^-\to K_S^0\barΞ^+Σ^-+\rm{c.c.}$ at 56 center-of-mass energies from 3.510 to 4.951~GeV. By fitting the dressed cross sections of $e^+e^-\to K_S^0\barΞ^+Σ^-+\rm{c.c.}$ with the assumption of a power-law function plus a charmonium(-like) resonance, i.e. $ψ(3770)$, $ψ(4040)$, $ψ(4160)$, $Y(4230)$, $Y(4360)$, $ψ(4415)$, {\it Y}(4500), $Y(4660)$, and {\it Y}(4710), no significant signal of any charmonium(-like) state decaying into the $K_S^0\barΞ^+Σ^-+\rm{c.c.}$ is observed. Upper limits on the product of the electronic width and branching fraction at the 90\% confidence level are given for each resonance. Combining this result with the previous measurement of the isospin-symmetric process $e^+e^-\to K^{-} \barΞ^{+} Σ^{0} + \rm{c.c.}$, the ratio of the Born cross sections, $R=σ^{B}(e^+e^-\to K_S^0\barΞ^+Σ^-+\rm{c.c.})/$$σ^{B}(e^+e^-\to K^-\barΞ^+Σ^0+\rm{c.c.})$, is found to be approximately 1.

</details>
