# hep-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [110] [Direct constraints on the agnostic $ν$SMEFT from heavy-neutrino searches at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21711)
*Lucía Duarte,Agustín Guillenea*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过对CMS实验同号双缪子加双喷注重希格斯中微子（heavy Majorana neutrinos）搜索进行详细重分析（recast），在维度六νSMEFT（neutrino Standard Model Effective Field Theory）框架下给出了首个来自LHC的agnostic参数空间实验排除限，有效耦合上限在5.8×10⁻⁷ GeV⁻²至2.3×10⁻⁶ GeV⁻²之间（对应质量200 GeV至15 TeV）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中微子振荡与质量的发现表明标准模型（Standard Model）需要扩展以引入右手型中微子（right-handed neutrinos）。若存在超出当前实验直接探测能量的新物理，其效应可通过中微子标准模型有效场论（νSMEFT）系统描述，其中重中性轻子（heavy neutral leptons）是对撞机搜索的重要目标。

Method: 本文重新解读（reinterpret）了CMS合作组对重马约拉纳中微子（heavy Majorana neutrinos）在同号双缪子（same-sign dimuon）加双喷注（two jets）末态中的搜索，通过详细重分析（recast）实验数据，对维度六（dimension-six）νSMEFT参数空间施加约束。研究采用了纯agnostic基准（pure-agnostic benchmark）和纳入无中微子双β衰变（neutrinoless double-beta decay）界限的基准。

Result: 在重中微子质量（heavy-neutrino mass）200 GeV至15 TeV范围内，观测到的有效耦合强度（effective coupling strength）上限在5.8×10⁻⁷ GeV⁻²到2.3×10⁻⁶ GeV⁻²之间。

Conclusion: 这是首次通过LHC重中微子搜索的专用重分析（dedicated recast）获得的对agnostic νSMEFT参数空间的实验排除限（experimental exclusion limits）。

Abstract: The observation of neutrino oscillations and masses motivates extensions of the Standard Model containing right-handed neutrinos. If additional new physics exists at scales beyond the direct reach of present experiments, its effects can be systematically described within the neutrino Standard Model Effective Field Theory ($ν$SMEFT). In this framework, heavy neutral leptons provide a promising target for collider searches. In this work, we reinterpret a CMS search for heavy Majorana neutrinos in the same-sign dimuon plus two jets final state to constrain the parameter space of the dimension-six $ν$SMEFT. Using a detailed recast of the experimental analysis, we present results for both a pure-agnostic benchmark and a benchmark incorporating neutrinoless double-beta-decay bounds. For heavy-neutrino masses in the range $200~\mathrm{GeV} \le m_N \le 15~\mathrm{TeV}$, the observed upper limits on the effective coupling strength range from $5.8\times10^{-7}\,\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}$ to $2.3\times10^{-6}\,\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}$. These results constitute the first experimental exclusion limits on the agnostic $ν$SMEFT parameter space obtained from a dedicated recast of an LHC heavy-neutrino search.

</details>


### [111] [ALP pair production at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21712)
*Ilaria Brivio,Simone Meoni,Davide Pagani*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究大型强子对撞机（LHC）中轴子类粒子（axion-like particle, ALP）对的产生，首次分析了非共振过程 $gg\to aa$ 的四光子末态，发现其可在300 fb$^{-1}$积分亮度下显著约束ALP相互作用；同时结合ATLAS合作组对希格斯共振过程 $gg\to h\to aa$ 的搜索进行重解释，揭示了多维参数空间中约束的非平凡特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对ALP相互作用的约束多集中于单一维度机制，缺乏对同时存在五维（dimension-5）和六维（dimension-6）ALP相互作用时多维参数空间的系统性分析。

Method: 聚焦于四孤立光子末态，首次研究非共振过程 $gg\to aa$；并将ATLAS合作组关于希格斯共振过程 $gg\to h\to aa$ 的搜索结果在三参数空间（three-parameter space）中进行重解释（recasting）。

Result: 非共振过程 $gg\to aa$ 在积分亮度为300 fb$^{-1}$时即可对ALP相互作用产生显著约束；非共振和希格斯共振两种ALP对产生过程所给出的多维约束展现出非平凡特征（non-trivial features）。

Conclusion: 本研究表明，多维参数空间中的ALP约束具有复杂结构，这些特征预期可推广至其他ALP衰变为标准模型粒子的搜索中。

Abstract: We study axion-like particle (ALP) pair production at the LHC, investigating its sensitivity to the simultaneous presence of dimension-5 and dimension-6 ALP interactions. Focusing on the signature with four isolated photons, we analyze for the first time the non-resonant process $gg\to aa$, finding that it can constrain significantly ALP interactions, already at an integrated luminosity of 300 fb$^{-1}$. Particular attention is paid to the multidimensional nature of the ALP parameter space. To this end, we present a re-interpretation of a search for the Higgs-resonant process $gg\to h\to aa$ by the ATLAS Collaboration, recasting their results within a three-parameter space. We find that the multi-dimensional constraints resulting from both non-resonant and Higgs-resonant ALP pair production exhibit non-trivial features, that are expected to extend to other searches in which the ALPs decay into Standard Model particles.

</details>


### [112] [Revisiting Quark Confinement in the Proton through the Force on Quarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21952)
*Ji-Xin Yu,Ao-Sheng Xiong,Ji Xu,Fu-Sheng Yu,Yong Zheng*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本研究利用光锥QCD求和规则（light-cone QCD sum rule）改进对质子内夸克力的定义与测量，将夸克力重建形式化为正则化逆问题（regularized inverse problem），从而减少模型依赖性，得到与线性QCD势（linear QCD potential）一致的结果，验证了现有框架的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 夸克禁闭（quark confinement）是标准模型（Standard Model）的核心问题之一。近期Ji等人提出了定义和测量质子内夸克力的框架，但该分析在实验数据与格点QCD信息有限的区域存在模型依赖问题。本文旨在克服这一局限性。

Method: 引入光锥QCD求和规则（light-cone QCD sum rule）以补充大$|q^2|$区域有限的实验与格点QCD信息；将夸克力的重建形式化为一个正则化逆问题（regularized inverse problem），从而减少此前预设函数参数化所带来的模型依赖。

Result: 得到的夸克力（quark force）是一种互补性的、较少依赖参数化的测定结果，且与线性QCD势（linear QCD potential）所隐含的结果一致，支持了Ji等人提出框架的鲁棒性。未来改进的输入数据可显著降低重建夸克力的不确定性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的正则化逆问题方法有效降低了夸克力重建中的模型依赖，验证了已有框架的可靠性，并为未来实验与理论输入精度的提升提供了明确方向。

Abstract: Quark confinement, the fact that colored quarks are permanently bound inside color-neutral hadrons and have never been observed as isolated particles, remains one of the central issues of the Standard Model. Recently, Ji et al.\,\cite{Ji:2026lyj} proposed a framework to define and measure the force on quarks in the proton, obtaining strong evidence for a net confining force and thus opening a new perspective on the study of confinement. In this work, we improve this analysis by incorporating light-cone QCD sum rule results to supplement the limited experimental and lattice QCD information in the large-$|q^2|$ region. We further formulate the reconstruction of the quark force as a regularized inverse problem, thereby reducing the model dependence associated with the prescribed functional parametrizations used before. The resulting quark force provides a complementary, less parametrization-dependent determination and remains consistent with that implied by a linear QCD potential, which also supports the robustness of the framework proposed in Ref.\,\cite{Ji:2026lyj}. We also show that improved future inputs can substantially reduce the uncertainty in the reconstructed quark force.

</details>


### [113] [Updated E141 constraints on a long-lived $X_{17}$ vector boson](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22102)
*A. Celentano,A. Marini,L. Marsicano*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文对SLAC E141束流倾倒实验（beam-dump experiment）针对X₁₇矢量玻色子（vector boson）的排除限（exclusion limit）进行了重新分析。通过更精确地处理实验设置与信号预测，修正了以往文献中基于简化假设得到的约束，并讨论了这些修正对ATOMKI异常（anomaly）的矢量玻色子解释与E141约束之间兼容性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2018年ATOMKI实验首次观测到的$^{8}$Be异常（$^{8}{\rm Be}$ anomaly）提示可能存在质量约16.9 MeV的新粒子X₁₇。然而，将X₁₇描述为矢量玻色子的最小模型（minimal model）受到过往束流倾倒实验零结果（null results）的严重约束。随着X₁₇质量被限制在很窄的区间，早期基于简化假设的排除限已不足以精确评估该场景。

Method: 本研究对E141实验的排除限进行了专门的重分析（dedicated reanalysis），在信号预测和实验设置的处理上采用了更高精度的方法，以替代以往文献中使用的简化近似。

Result: 量化了精细化处理对排除参数空间（excluded parameter space）的影响。E141实验对X₁₇与电子的耦合$\varepsilon_e$施加了$5.1\times10^{-5}\lesssim \varepsilon_e\lesssim1.7\times10^{-4}$区域的严格约束，排除了长寿命X₁₇的可能性，这与ATOMKI合作组近期对粒子寿命（particle lifetime）的初步估计可能存在矛盾。

Conclusion: 讨论了修正后的E141约束与X₁₇作为矢量玻色子解释ATOMKI异常之间的兼容性问题，强调了精细化重分析对于评估该新粒子场景的重要意义。

Abstract: Since its first observation by the ATOMKI experiment in 2018, the $\rm ^*Be$ anomaly has attracted considerable interest within the dark sector community as it may indicate the existence of a new fundamental particle with a mass of about 16.9 MeV, the $X_{17}$. However, the minimal model describing $X_{17}$ as a new vector boson is severely constrained by null results from legacy beam-dump experiments. Among these, the E141 experiment at SLAC places stringent limits on the $X_{17}$ coupling to electrons $\varepsilon_e$ in the $5.1\times10^{-5}\lesssim \varepsilon_e\lesssim1.7\times10^{-4}$ region. This excludes the possibility of a long-lived $X_{17}$, potentially in contrast with the preliminary estimate of the particle lifetime recently reported by the ATOMKI collaboration. The E141 limits commonly adopted in the literature rely on reinterpretations of the original analysis under solid but simplifying assumptions. While these studies provide a reliable estimate of the experiment's reach, they rely on approximations that were well justified when the boson mass was largely unconstrained. With the $X_{17}$ mass now confined to a narrow region by recent experimental observations, a more refined treatment of the E141 sensitivity becomes necessary. In this work, we revisit the E141 exclusion limits in the $X_{17}$ scenario by performing a dedicated reanalysis that incorporates a more accurate treatment of the experimental setup and signal prediction. We quantify the impact of these refinements on the excluded parameter space and discuss their implications for the compatibility between the E141 constraints and the vector boson interpretation of the ATOMKI anomalies.

</details>


### [114] [Statistical validation of template-based parameter extraction from toy thrust distributions for future FCC-ee studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22282)
*Fatih ilgin*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文开发并验证了一套基于模板的统计推断框架，用于未来FCC-ee实验中的事件形状（event-shape）研究。通过受控的toy推力分布（thrust distributions）作为方法学基准，系统比较了三种参数提取策略（离散卡方距离、离散多项负对数似然和线性插值似然），在200至2000事件的样本规模下评估其统计表现。结果表明多项式似然一致优于基准方法，而模型失配（model mismatch）会引入可测偏差，说明仅有名义闭合（nominal closure）不足以确保方法稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠参数提取过程需要独立于其最终要应用的物理模型和探测器模型进行验证。该研究旨在为FCC-ee的未来事件形状研究建立一个受控的方法学基准（methodological benchmark），以系统比较不同统计推断策略的性能。

Method: 采用受控的二喷注类（two-jet-like）和三喷注类（three-jet-like）事件组分的toy推力分布，其相对贡献由连续控制参数决定。比较了三种提取策略：离散Pearson型卡方距离（discrete Pearson-type χ² distance）、离散多项负对数似然估计（discrete multinomial negative-log-likelihood estimator）以及基于线性插值模板的似然估计（likelihood estimator based on linearly interpolated templates）。通过10,000次伪实验（pseudo-experiments）在200至2000事件的样本上量化性能，并测试了离网恢复（off-grid recovery）、直方图分箱变化、模板统计量变化以及基于修正动量涂抹的模型失配测试。

Result: 多项式似然估计一致优于简单不相关Pearson型基线。对于输入参数0.30，正确模板分数（correct-template fraction）从200事件的0.467±0.005提升至2000事件的0.934±0.002。插值减少了离网离散化效应，在测试配置中最大绝对平均偏差约为0.003。模型失配产生了可测量的偏差。

Conclusion: 名义闭合不足以确保方法稳健性。该研究未确定物理强耦合常数（strong coupling constant），但为未来应用于真实部分子簇射模拟（parton-shower simulations）、强子化模型（hadronization models）、探测器效应和FCC-ee数据之前，提供了一个比较估计量、量化统计分辨率（statistical resolution）和诊断模型依赖性的受控框架。

Abstract: A statistically reliable parameter-extraction procedure should be validated independently of the physical and detector models to which it will eventually be applied. A template-based inference framework is developed and tested using controlled toy thrust distributions as a methodological benchmark for future FCC-ee event-shape studies. The model contains two-jet-like and three-jet-like event components whose relative contribution is governed by a continuous control parameter. Three extraction strategies are compared: a discrete Pearson-type $χ^2$ distance, a discrete multinomial negative-log-likelihood estimator, and a likelihood estimator based on linearly interpolated templates. Their performance is quantified with 10,000 pseudo-experiments for samples containing 200--2000 events. Off-grid recovery, histogram-binning variations, template-statistics variations, and a model-mismatch test based on modified momentum smearing are also studied. The multinomial likelihood consistently outperforms the simple uncorrelated Pearson-type baseline used here. For an input parameter of 0.30, the correct-template fraction increases from $0.467\pm0.005$ for 200 events to $0.934\pm0.002$ for 2000 events. Interpolation reduces off-grid discretization effects, with a maximum absolute mean bias of approximately 0.003 in the tested configuration. Model mismatch nevertheless produces measurable biases, demonstrating that nominal closure alone is insufficient to establish robustness. This study does not determine the physical strong coupling constant. It provides a controlled framework for comparing estimators, quantifying statistical resolution, and diagnosing model dependence before application to realistic parton-shower simulations, hadronization models, detector effects, and FCC-ee data.

</details>


### [115] [Scattering Amplitudes as Programs: Self-Evolving Search for Theory and Event Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21629)
*Yi Gu,Sven Krippendorf*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过将散射振幅（scattering amplitudes）视为计算机程序，将揭示解析结构与构建高效数值评估器两个目标联系起来。研究在外部生成-评估-选择循环中嵌入仓库级编码智能体，针对缩放搜索、结构搜索和精确运算搜索三个优化目标，实现了从BCFW递归到专用固定k分裂螺旋度传输算法的转变（805倍加速）、NMHV项的重组以减少项间抵消，以及20个QCD和电弱过程中47.7倍的算术运算缩减。在纯胶子分量测试中，进化引擎相对于Sherpa-Comix的加速比从10倍增至277倍，与MadGraph5_aMC@NLO Fortran编译版本的差距从77.7倍缩小至13.7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连接散射振幅中解析结构暴露与数值评估器构建两个目标，认识到等效振幅程序在多重性缩放、算术复杂度、抵消和运行时间上存在显著差异，从而将振幅计算定义为一个结构化的搜索问题。

Method: 将仓库级编码智能体（repository-scale coding agents）嵌入外部生成-评估-选择循环（generate-evaluate-select loop），使用冻结评估器和目标函数，研究缩放搜索（scaling search）、结构搜索（structural search）和精确运算搜索（exact-operation search）三个优化目标。搜索在数学表示之间移动，结合递归、对称性、基约化、动态规划和共享计算，形成混合振幅程序（hybrid amplitude programs）。

Result: 缩放搜索实现805倍几何平均加速；结构搜索将NMHV项重组为R-不变性类单元和胶合超单元，减少项间抵消；精确运算搜索在20个QCD和电弱过程中减少47.7倍算术运算，带来5.9倍Python运行时间提升。在纯胶子分量测试中（n=4至n=6），进化引擎相对于Sherpa-Comix的加速比从10倍增至277倍，与MadGraph5_aMC@NLO Fortran（-O3编译）的差距从77.7倍缩小至13.7倍。

Conclusion: 研究表明振幅优化的部分环节可以通过自进化程序搜索（self-evolving program search）系统化实现。原生生成器集成和端到端事件吞吐量留待未来验证；生成器对比仅针对孤立的精确矩阵元调用，未修改MadGraph或Sherpa本身。

Abstract: By viewing scattering amplitudes as computer programs, we connect two goals: exposing useful analytic structure and constructing efficient numerical evaluators for collider phenomenology. Equivalent programs can differ sharply in multiplicity scaling, arithmetic complexity, cancellation, and runtime. Amplitude calculation therefore defines a structured search problem over analytic representations, recursive algorithms, colour and helicity organisation, and reuse of intermediate objects.
  We embed repository-scale coding agents inside an external generate-evaluate-select loop with frozen evaluators and objectives, and study three optimisation targets. A scaling search moves from BCFW recursion to a specialised fixed-k split-helicity transfer algorithm, reaching an 805x geometric-mean speed-up on the scored grid. A structural search reorganises NMHV terms into R-invariant-style cells and glued supercells, reducing inter-term cancellation. Across twenty QCD and electroweak processes, an exact-operation search reduces counted arithmetic by 47.7x and gives a 5.9x post-hoc Python runtime improvement. In matched pure-gluon component tests from n=4 to n=6, the evolved engine's speed advantage over the tested Sherpa-Comix exact-sum call grows from 10x to 277x, while its gap to process-specialised MadGraph5_aMC@NLO Fortran compiled at -O3 narrows from 77.7x to 13.7x.
  The searches move between mathematical representations and combine recursion, symmetry, basis reduction, dynamic programming, and shared computation into hybrid amplitude programs. They provide initial evidence that parts of amplitude optimisation can be made systematic through self-evolving program search, while leaving native generator integration and end-to-end event throughput as future tests. The generator comparison is an isolated exact matrix-element call, not a modification of MadGraph or Sherpa.

</details>


### [116] [Hot and Dense Medium Effects on the $B_s^*$ and $B^*$ Multiplets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22419)
*K. Azizi,N. Er,J. Y. Süngü*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用有限温度与密度下的QCD求和规则（QCD sum rules），系统分析了$B_s^*(5415)$和$B^*(5325)$多重态（包括正反粒子）在介质中的质量与衰变常数。结果表明，质量对介质环境表现出显著抵抗性（极端条件下损失不超过$\sim 13\%$），而衰变常数极为敏感（可损失高达$\sim 78\%$）。重子密度主导介质响应，温度仅当系统趋近退禁闭相变时才起次要作用。在有限重子密度下，正反粒子之间出现明显的不对称性，这为未来的重离子对撞实验提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解底夸克矢量介子在有限温度与密度介质中的性质变化，对于解释重离子对撞实验（如RHIC、LHC、FAIR、NICA）中产生的强子信号至关重要。本文旨在系统计算$B_s^*$和$B^*$多重态在极端环境下质量与衰变常数的介质修正，填补正反粒子不对称性研究的空白。

Method: 采用有限温度与有限密度下的QCD求和规则（QCD sum rules），在算子乘积展开（OPE）中完整包含了夸克凝聚、胶子凝聚和混合凝聚的温度与密度依赖贡献，分别计算了奇异（$B_s^{*0}$, $\bar{B}_s^{*0}$）、带电（$B^{*\pm}$）和中性（$B^{*0}$, $\bar{B}^{*0}$）双重态的性质。

Result: 在整个多重态中，质量对介质表现出显著抵抗性，即使在$T = T_c$和$n = 5n_0$的极端条件下，任何态的质量损失也不超过$\sim 13\%$；而衰变常数更为敏感，在相同条件下可损失高达$\sim 78\%$。重子密度主导介质响应，温度仅当系统趋近退禁闭转变时起次要作用。零密度下各态的质量移动在$-(0.5$-$1.1)\%$之间，衰变常数移动在$-(3.9$-$5.3)\%$之间；有限重子密度下出现明显的正反粒子不对称性，例如在$T = 0, n = 5n_0$时，$\bar{B}^{*0}$质量下降$12.9\%$，而$B^{*0}$仅下降$6.1\%$，该差距完全由矢量自能驱动。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了底矢量介子在介质中质量与衰变常数的系统性认识，揭示了正反粒子在有限重子密度下的显著不对称性，为RHIC、LHC、FAIR、NICA等未来重离子对撞实验提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We present an extensive analysis of the in-medium masses and decay constants of the $B_s^*(5415)$ and $B^*(5325)$ multiplets, including both particles and antiparticles, using QCD sum rules at finite temperature and density. The OPE incorporates the full temperature- and density-dependent contributions from the quark, gluon, and mixed condensates. Computing the strange ($B_s^{*0}$, $\bar{B}_s^{*0}$), charged ($B^{*\pm}$), and neutral ($B^{*0}$, $\bar{B}^{*0}$) doublet properties allows us to study the effects of flavor symmetry breaking, strangeness, and heavy-quark decoupling on the beauty vector mesons in the medium. Our results indicate that the mass is remarkably resistant to the medium across the entire multiplet: no state loses more than $\sim 13\%$ of its vacuum value, even at $T = T_c$ and $n = 5n_0$, the extreme conditions explored here. The decay constant is far more sensitive, losing up to $\sim 78\%$ at the same point. Baryon density clearly dominates the medium response, while temperature plays a secondary role until the system approaches the deconfinement crossover. At zero density, every state loses almost the same fraction of its mass and decay constant: mass shifts lie between $-(0.5$-$1.1)\%$ and decay-constant shifts between $-(3.9$-$5.3)\%$, regardless of charge or flavor, so temperature alone does not distinguish a particle from its antiparticle. At finite baryon density, a clear particle-antiparticle asymmetry emerges: at $T = 0$ and $n = 5n_0$, the $\bar{B}^{*0}$ mass decreases by $12.9\%$, whereas the $B^{*0}$ mass shifts by only $6.1\%$, a gap of nearly seven percentage points driven entirely by the vector self-energy. This provides a theoretical basis for the future heavy-ion collision program at RHIC, LHC, FAIR, and NICA.

</details>


### [117] [Formation and scaling of $\mathbb{Z}_N$ strings for global $\mathrm{SU}(N)/\mathbb{Z}_N$ symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21701)
*Masaki Yamada*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过经典晶格模拟，研究了PSU(N)真空流形上具有多弦结的全局Z_N弦网络的形成与演化。对于N=2至8，网络进入标度区（scaling regime）而非阻挫态（frustrated），弦密度归一化正比于伴随表示维数N²−1，并由此推导出引力波能谱幅度的标度律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该模型的构造源于质量形变N=4超对称杨-米尔斯理论（即N=1*理论）的希格斯真空（Higgs vacua），旨在提供一个可解的有效描述，用于研究带有重子顶点（baryon-vertex）类结的弦网络。

Method: 采用经典晶格模拟（classical lattice simulations），在辐射主导宇宙（radiation-dominated universe）中研究这些弦网络的形成与演化过程。选取N=2,3,4,5,8进行数值分析。

Result: 对于所有研究的N值，网络均进入标度区而非阻挫态；弦密度归一化正比于N²−1；非最小荷组分（non-minimal-charge components）保持次主导地位。

Conclusion: 对于N≲8，宇宙弦网络产生的引力波能量密度幅度标度为Ω_GW∝μ²(N²−1)²，其中μ为单位荷弦的张力（tension）。

Abstract: We numerically investigate networks of global $\mathbb{Z}_N$ strings with multi-string junctions in a scalar field model whose vacuum manifold is $\mathrm{PSU}(N)=\mathrm{SU}(N)/\mathbb{Z}_N$. The construction of the model is motivated by the Higgs vacua of mass-deformed $\mathcal{N}=4$ supersymmetric Yang-Mills theory, commonly known as $\mathcal{N}=1^*$ theory, and provides a tractable effective description of string networks with baryon-vertex-like junctions. We perform classical lattice simulations of the formation and evolution of these networks in a radiation-dominated universe. For $N=2,3,4,5,$ and $8$, we find that the networks approach a scaling regime rather than becoming frustrated. The normalization of the string density grows in proportion to the dimension of the adjoint representation, $N^2-1$, while non-minimal-charge components remain subdominant. These results imply that, at least for $N\lesssim 8$, the amplitude of the gravitational-wave energy density generated by the cosmic-string network scales as $Ω_{\rm GW}\propto μ^2 (N^2-1)^2$, where $μ$ is the tension of a unit-charge string.

</details>


### [118] [Non-Markovian heavy-quark equilibration and equilibrium correlation function in a thermal medium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22403)
*Monisha Nair,Juan Torres-Rincon,Santosh K. Das*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在朗之万（Langevin）框架下计算粲夸克（charm quark）流-流关联函数，并利用格林-久保（Green-Kubo）公式提取重夸克扩散系数。研究发现，在非相对论极限下，记忆效应虽定性改变流关联的瞬态结构，但输运系数保持不变；同时，非马尔可夫（non-Markovian）浴中的记忆效应可导致重夸克出现阻尼振荡式的平衡化过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解重夸克在量子色动力学（QCD）介质中的输运性质，特别是扩散系数，对于描述夸克-胶子等离子体（QGP）中的重夸克行为至关重要。本文旨在考察记忆效应对重夸克电流关联函数及扩散系数计算的影响，并进一步研究记忆效应对重夸克热化过程的作用。

Method: 采用朗之万框架（Langevin framework）计算粲夸克流-流关联函数，并通过格林-久保公式（Green-Kubo formula）提取扩散系数。将形式论推广至广义朗之万方程（generalized Langevin equation），引入指数衰减的记忆核（memory kernel）以纳入记忆效应。在非相对论极限下，求解广义福克-普朗克方程（generalized Fokker-Planck equation）的非平衡解，与广义朗之万方程结果进行比较。此外，还考虑了相对论性关联噪声（correlated noise）情形。

Result: 记忆效应定性改变了流关联的瞬态结构，但在非相对论极限下，输运系数（扩散系数）的值保持不变。记忆效应可导致重夸克在非马尔可夫浴（non-Markovian bath）中出现阻尼、振荡式的平衡化过程。

Conclusion: 记忆效应不影响非相对论极限下由格林-久保公式提取的重夸克扩散系数，但会显著改变系统的瞬态关联与热化路径，这提示在描述重夸克在QGP中的非平衡动力学时，需要考虑记忆效应带来的定性修正。

Abstract: We compute the charm-quark current-current correlation function within the Langevin framework and extract the heavy-quark diffusion coefficient using the Green--Kubo formula. The formalism is further extended to evaluate the correlation function using a generalized Langevin equation that incorporates memory effects via an exponentially decaying memory kernel. We find that while memory effects qualitatively modify the transient structure of the current correlations, the value of the transport coefficient remains unchanged in the non-relativistic limit. In addition, we investigate heavy-quark thermalization in the presence of memory and compare the non-equilibrium solution of a generalized Fokker--Planck equation with the one obtained from the generalized Langevin equation in the non-relativistic limit. We also consider the relativistic version of the correlated noise case and observe that memory effects can give rise to a damped, oscillatory equilibration of the heavy quark in a non-Markovian bath.

</details>

### [119] [Universal scaling of electrochemical information transfer at solid-liquid interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22190)
*Eikichi Kimura,Ashutosh Rathi,Saki Tsuchiya,El Mustapha Mansouri,Sara Mandić,Kristi Kanya Saikia,Keisuke Oshimi,Masazumi Fujiwara,Keigo Arai*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 本文发现固液界面电化学势的影响在固体内部遵循由单一无量纲因子 $u$ 控制的普适反平方衰减律（universal inverse-square scaling），该衰减与探针的微观细节无关，并将静电屏蔽（electrostatic screening）确立为固液界面信息传递的基本约束，为地下电化学传感（subsurface electrochemical sensing）提供定量设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固液界面的电化学势（electrochemical potentials）主导着众多化学与能量转换过程，但其电化学影响在固体内部能延伸多远尚不清楚。

Method: 通过定义一个无量纲因子 $u$（液体与探针位置之间的有效静电分离距离与固体中静电传播长度（electrostatic propagation length）之比），推导出电化学信息可达性的普适反平方标度律。

Result: 最大可获取的电化学信息受单一无量纲因子 $u$ 控制，且其衰减遵循与探针微观细节无关的普适反平方律。

Conclusion: 静电屏蔽是固液界面上信息传递的基本约束，该发现为地下电化学传感提供了定量设计原则。

Abstract: Electrochemical potentials at solid-liquid interfaces govern diverse chemical and energy conversion processes; however, the extent to which their electrochemical influence extends into the solid remains unclear. This study demonstrates that the maximally accessible electrochemical information is controlled by a single dimensionless factor $u$, defined as the ratio of the effective electrostatic separation between the liquid and probe locations to the electrostatic propagation length in the solid. The resulting universal inverse-square scaling is independent of the microscopic details of the probe. This attenuation law identifies electrostatic screening as a fundamental constraint on information transfer across solid-liquid interfaces, providing quantitative design principles for subsurface electrochemical sensing.

</details>

### [120] [Global Finite-Energy Weak Solutions and Sharp Entropy Decay for a Poisson-Nernst-Planck System with Interspecies Drag and Steric Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21742)
*Baoli Hao,Fanze Kong,Kei Fong Lam,Chun Liu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文通过能量变分方法（energetic variational approach）推导并分析了一个包含位阻相互作用（steric interactions）和种间拖拽（interspecies drag）的二元Poisson-Nernst-Planck系统，证明了全局有限能量弱解的存在性，建立了熵-熵产生不等式，并获得了指数松弛行为及线性化最优常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Poisson-Nernst-Planck（PNP）模型未同时考虑位阻效应和种间拖拽机制，而这两种因素在带电粒子输运中具有重要物理意义。本文旨在通过严格的数学分析，填补该耦合系统在解的存在性、松弛行为及熵产生结构方面的理论空白。

Method: 采用能量变分方法（energetic variational approach）将位阻效应纳入自由能（free energy），将拖拽机制引入耗散泛函（dissipation functional）。通过消去输运速度，得到非对角、浓度依赖的Onsager迁移率（Onsager mobility）以及非$L^2(0,T;H^1)$ coercive的熵产生结构。利用熵变量近似（entropy-variable approximation）、加权梯度估计（weighted gradient estimates）和真空兼容的平方根公式（vacuum-compatible square-root formulation）证明全局有限能量弱解的存在性；在纯Neumann等质量情形下建立子水平熵-熵产生不等式（sublevel entropy-entropy production inequality），并推导线性化最优常数。

Result: 证明了拖拽修正位阻PNP系统全局有限能量弱解的存在性；在纯Neumann等质量设定下，获得了近似生成弱解的指数松弛（exponential relaxation）；识别了最优熵产生常数（optimal entropy-production constant）的尖锐小子水平极限（sharp small-sublevel limit），该常数通过拖拽迁移率、位阻Hessian矩阵、Poisson耦合和Neumann谱的显式线性化公式给出。该线性化常数同样控制了均匀平衡附近小强扰动的局部非线性松弛。秩一位阻极限与位阻矩阵正定性在有限能量紧致理论中的作用也得到了讨论。

Conclusion: 本文从理论和数学上建立了包含位阻和拖拽效应的PNP系统的解的存在性与长时间行为，揭示了熵产生常数与系统各组分的线性化结构之间的精确关系，为后续研究提供了严谨的分析框架。

Abstract: We derive and analyze a binary Poisson-Nernst-Planck system with steric interactions and interspecies drag through the energetic variational approach. The steric effects are incorporated into the free energy, while the drag mechanism enters the dissipation functional; eliminating the transport velocities yields a non-diagonal, concentration-dependent Onsager mobility and an entropy-production structure that is not coercive in the standard $L^2(0,T;H^1)$ sense. For the resulting drag-modified steric PNP system, we prove the existence of global finite-energy weak solutions using an entropy-variable approximation, weighted gradient estimates, and a vacuum-compatible square-root formulation of the weighted entropy gradients. In the pure Neumann equal-mass setting, we establish a sublevel entropy-entropy production inequality, obtain exponential relaxation for approximation-generated weak solutions, and identify the sharp small-sublevel limit of the optimal entropy-production constant through an explicit linearized formula involving the drag mobility, steric Hessian, Poisson coupling, and Neumann spectrum. We further show that the same linearized constant governs the local nonlinear relaxation of sufficiently small strong perturbations of the homogeneous equilibrium. Finally, we discuss the rank-one steric limit and clarify the role of the positive definiteness of the steric matrix in the finite-energy compactness theory.

</details>


### [121] [A PDE approach to the 2D Yang-Mills measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22236)
*Ilya Chevyrev,Tom Klose,Abdulwahab Mohamed*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出粗糙可加函数（rough additive functions）的概念，将粗糙路径理论（rough paths theory）推广至分布型1-形式的线积分。在规范场论（gauge theory）背景下，利用该概念定义受控规范变换（controlled gauge transformations）和通过粗糙微分方程（RDEs）定义的完整群（holonomies）。主要结果是基于粗糙可加函数的分布型联络的Uhlenbeck紧致性粗糙版本。关键工具是通过奇异椭圆偏微分方程（singular elliptic PDE）获得库仑规范（Coulomb gauge），该方程借助正则性结构（regularity structures）和受隐函数定理启发的方法求解。模型完全由粗糙可加函数决定，形式更简单且几何更自然。将该理论应用于单位正方形上的杨-米尔斯测度（Yang-Mills measure），证明其具有最优正则性的规范固定表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有粗糙路径理论主要处理连续路径的积分，缺乏对分布型1-形式（distributional 1-forms）线积分的系统处理。在规范场论中，分布型联络（distributional connections）的紧致性分析缺少粗糙版本的Uhlenbeck紧致性定理，且杨-米尔斯测度的规范固定表示尚未达到最优正则性。

Method: 引入粗糙可加函数作为核心概念，将粗糙路径理论扩展到分布型1-形式的线积分。通过粗糙微分方程定义受控规范变换和完整群。求解奇异椭圆偏微分方程以得到库仑规范，求解过程中结合正则性结构（regularity structures）与受隐函数定理（implicit function theorem）启发的方法。所依赖的模型完全由粗糙可加函数决定。

Result: 证明了基于粗糙可加函数的分布型联络的Uhlenbeck紧致性粗糙版本。给出了单位正方形上杨-米尔斯测度的规范固定表示，并证明其具有最优正则性（optimal regularity）。求解奇异椭圆PDE所需的模型完全由粗糙可加函数确定，比现有方法更简单且几何更自然。

Conclusion: 粗糙可加函数为分布型联络的分析提供了更简单且几何更自然的框架。尽管本文聚焦于单位正方形，但所发展的方法预期可推广至更一般的曲面（surfaces）上，为杨-米尔斯测度及相关规范场论问题的进一步研究奠定基础。

Abstract: We introduce the concept of rough additive functions, which extends rough paths theory to line integrals of distributional 1-forms. In the context of gauge theory, we use this notion to define controlled gauge transformations and holonomies via RDEs. One of our main results is a rough version of Uhlenbeck compactness for distributional connections based on rough additive functions. The main ingredient is a singular elliptic PDE to obtain a Coulomb gauge. We solve this PDE using regularity structures and a method inspired by the implicit function theorem. Surprisingly, the model needed to solve the equation is determined entirely from rough additive functions, which is a simpler and geometrically more natural object. We apply our results to the Yang-Mills measure on the unit square, showing that it has a gauge-fixed representation with optimal regularity. Although we focus on the unit square in this article, we expect our results to apply to more general surfaces.

</details>


### [122] [The semiclassical limit from Hartree to Vlasov at positive density: strong uniform-in-time convergence and scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22490)
*Marnie Smith*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在三维空间中建立了从 Hartree 方程到 Vlasov 方程的强半经典收敛性，在 Penrose 稳定的均匀稳态附近证明了 $O(\hbar^2)$ 的收敛速率，并结合一致相混合与散射界，实现了 Vlasov 解的全局存在性与散射；在初始扰动对齐时，收敛对所有时间一致，散射轮廓的 Wigner 变换以 $O((\ln\hbar^{-1})^{-1/3})$ 的显式速率收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立 Hartree 方程（Hartree equation）到 Vlasov 方程（Vlasov equation）在物理相关稳态附近的强半经典收敛性，并量化收敛速率，以连接量子与经典动力学。

Method: 利用加权 Sobolev 空间（weighted Sobolev spaces）中的估计，结合先前的关于 Hartree 方程的一致相混合（uniform-in-ħ phase-mixing）与散射界（scattering bounds），通过半经典极限（semiclassical limit）论证 Vlasov 解的全局存在性与散射。

Result: 在 Penrose 稳定的均匀稳态（Penrose-stable homogeneous steady states）附近，对于充分正则的可积相互作用核，在有限时间区间上证明了 $O(\hbar^2)$ 收敛速率；初始扰动对齐时，半经典收敛对所有时间一致，且 Wigner 变换（Wigner transforms）的量子散射轮廓以显式速率 $O((\ln\hbar^{-1})^{-1/3})$ 收敛到经典散射轮廓。

Conclusion: 在正密度条件下，Hartree 到 Vlasov 的强半经典收敛性得以严格建立，收敛速率由参数 $\hbar$ 与控制精度显式给出；该结果为量子-经典动力学对应提供了定量的理论保证。

Abstract: Strong semiclassical convergence from the Hartree equation to the Vlasov equation is established in three dimensions near Penrose-stable homogeneous steady states at positive density. For sufficiently regular integrable interaction kernels, an $O(\hbar^2)$ convergence rate in weighted Sobolev spaces is proved on finite time intervals. Combining this estimate with earlier uniform-in-$\hbar$ phase-mixing and scattering bounds for the Hartree equation yields global existence and scattering for the corresponding Vlasov solution through the semiclassical limit. If the quantum and classical initial perturbations are aligned, the semiclassical convergence is uniform for all times, and the Wigner transforms of the quantum scattering profiles converge to the classical scattering profile at the explicit rate $O((\ln\hbar^{-1})^{-1/3})$.

</details>

### [123] [Counting Edge Modes with the Higher Berry Curvature: A Bulk Topological Order Parameter for Quantum Spin Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21705)
*Adam J. McRoberts,Joe Crossley,Chris Hooley,Joe H. Winter*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文证明高阶贝里曲率（higher Berry curvature, HBC）可以作为量子自旋链的整数拓扑序参量，通过计数纠缠切割产生的无能隙边缘模的自旋来刻画相变，并在多种自旋链模型中进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为量子自旋链寻找能够计数纠缠切割产生的无能隙边缘模、并定义整数拓扑序参量的方法。

Method: 通过插值到参考乘积 Néel 态的方式构造扩展族（extending family），证明高阶贝里曲率（HBC）在该扩展上的积分等于半链在无穷小场响应下的普通贝里相位（Berry phase）。

Result: HBC 积分计数了切割暴露的无能隙边缘模的自旋，其整数值的变化标志着相变；在 S=1/2、S=1、S=3/2 自旋-佩尔斯链、双线性-双二次链以及交错 J1-J2 链等多个模型中均得到验证。

Conclusion: 高阶贝里曲率（HBC）可作为量子自旋链的整数拓扑序参量，并澄清了与严格对称性保护拓扑相（symmetry-protected topological phases）分类的关联。

Abstract: We show that the higher Berry curvature (HBC) can be used to count the gapless edge modes created by an entanglement cut, and thus defines an integer-valued topological order parameter for quantum spin chains. Given an individual spin-chain Hamiltonian, we construct an extending family by interpolating to a reference product Néel state, and show that the integral of the HBC over this extension is equal to the ordinary Berry phase of half of the chain swept out in response to an \textit{infinitesimal} field. It thus counts the spin of the gapless edge modes exposed by the cut, and a change in its integer value signals a phase transition. We illustrate this with several examples: $S=1/2$, $S=1$, and $S=3/2$ spin-Peierls chains, which undergo `singlet flop' transitions between different patterns of dimerisation; the bilinear-biquadratic chain, which clarifies the connection to the strict symmetry-protected topological phases classification; and the staggered $J_1$--$J_2$ chain, which has both nearest-neighbour and third-neighbour patterns of singlets depending on the signs of the interactions.

</details>


### [124] [Intrinsic single crystals of MnTe altermagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21803)
*Kim-Khuong Huynh,Michael Anthony Quintero,Martin Klanjšek,Tilen Knaflič,Yuta Ishii,Norimasa Sasabe,Nhu-Quynh T. Phan,Frej Søren Rattenborg,Yuichi Yamasaki,Denis Arčon,Bo Brummerstedt Iversen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文报道了典型交替磁体（altermagnet）MnTe超高纯单晶的合成方法、结构及本征物性。自助熔剂法生长的晶体近乎无缺陷与非化学计量比；在准独立构型测量下，晶体在交替磁转变（TN≈310 K）附近表现出复杂各向异性畴动力学；低温（TMI≈150 K）出现安德森绝缘态（Anderson insulating state），载流子密度约1.6×10¹⁷ cm⁻³，与缺Te样品通常呈现的金属态形成鲜明对比。交替磁性的标志——反常霍尔效应（anomalous Hall effect）和X射线磁圆二色性（XMCD）信号在本征极限下依然稳健，但幅度显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成超高纯MnTe单晶并测量其本征物性，以阐明交替磁体（altermagnet）在极限纯净条件下依然保持交替磁性特征（如反常霍尔效应和XMCD信号），同时揭示其与缺Te样品截然不同的电输运行为（安德森绝缘态 vs. 金属态）。

Method: 采用自助熔剂法（self-flux method）生长超高纯MnTe单晶；通过多种表征手段确认晶体近乎无缺陷与非化学计量比；在准独立构型（quasi free-standing configuration）下进行测量以最小化应力效应。

Result: 晶体在交替磁转变（TN=310 K）附近表现出复杂各向异性畴动力学（domain kinetics），涵盖超热（superheating）和过冷（supercooling）区域；在TMI≈150 K以下出现安德森绝缘态，载流子密度约1.6×10¹⁷ cm⁻³；反常霍尔效应和XMCD信号依然稳健，但幅度显著降低。

Conclusion: 超高纯MnTe单晶的本征态呈现安德森绝缘态而非金属态，但交替磁性的核心特征（反常霍尔效应、XMCD信号）在本征极限下仍然存在，只是强度减弱。这一结果表明交替磁性具有鲁棒性，但本征无序或缺陷浓度对电输运和磁响应有显著影响。

Abstract: We report the synthesis methodology, structure, and intrinsic properties of ultra-high quality single crystals of MnTe, an archetypal altermagnet. The crystals, obtained from self-flux method, are nearly free from crystal imperfections and disproportionate chemical compositions as seen by various investigation methods. In measurements under quasi free-standing configuration minimizing stress induced effects, the crystals exhibit complex and anisotropic domain kinetics in both superheating and supercooling regimes around the altermagnetic transition at $T_{\mathrm{N}} = 310\,\mathrm{K}$. An Anderson insulating state is observed below $T_{\mathrm{MI}}\approx 150\,\mathrm{K}$ with a carrier density of about $1.6\times 10^{17}\,\mathrm{cm}^{-3}$, being sharply contrast to metallic states usually seen in Te-deficit samples. Nevertheless, hallmarks of altermagnetism, anomalous Hall effect and X-ray magnetic circular dichroism signal, are robust in this intrinsic limit, however with significantly reduced magnitudes.

</details>


### [125] [Hydrodynamic Memory and Long-Time Tails in Clean Frustrated Magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21827)
*Yufei Pei,Claudio Castelnovo,Roderich Moessner*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文在阻挫磁体（frustrated magnet）中发现了一个具有记忆效应的自相互作用随机游走（self-interacting random walk）。单极子（monopole）——自旋冰（spin ice）中的一种分数化准粒子（fractionalized quasiparticle）——的运动表现出缓慢的弛豫，其速度自相关函数（velocity autocorrelation function）呈现$t^{-3/2}$的代数长时尾（algebraic long-time tail）。在有限单极子密度下，不同单极子尾迹间的相互作用引入新的时间尺度，导致长时尾被指数截断（exponential cutoff）。该研究将洁净阻挫磁体确立为研究自相互作用随机过程（self-interacting stochastic processes）和水动力学记忆（hydrodynamic memory）的微观平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别洁净阻挫磁体（clean frustrated magnet）中一种受约束的、具有记忆效应的自相互作用随机游走，从而为研究非马尔可夫（non-Markovian）准粒子输运提供微观物理平台。

Method: 对单个单极子在自旋冰中的运动进行分析，计算其速度自相关函数；再考虑有限单极子密度下不同单极子尾迹（trails）之间的相互作用，研究其对弛豫行为的影响。

Result: 单个单极子运动的速度自相关函数表现出$t^{-3/2}$的代数长时尾，弛豫极为缓慢；有限密度下，单极子之间的尾迹相互作用引入额外时间尺度，使该长时尾被指数截断。

Conclusion: 洁净阻挫磁体可作为微观平台，用于研究自相互作用随机过程、水动力学记忆以及从局域马尔可夫动力学（local Markovian dynamics）涌现出的非马尔可夫准粒子输运。

Abstract: In a simple, clean but constrained magnet, we identify a self-interacting random walk with memory. For the motion of a single monopole -- a fractionalized quasiparticle in spin ice -- this produces a subtle and unusually slow relaxation toward diffusive motion. This is manifested as an algebraic long-time tail in the velocity autocorrelation function, decaying as $t^{-3/2}$. At finite monopole density, the interactions between the trails of different monopoles introduce an additional timescale, corresponding to the disruption of a monopole's memory by other monopoles, and leading to an exponential cutoff of the long-time tail. Our results identify clean frustrated magnets as microscopic platforms for studying self-interacting stochastic processes, hydrodynamic memory, and the emergence of non-Markovian quasiparticle transport from local Markovian dynamics.

</details>


### [126] [Quasi-Two-Dimensional Quantum Antiferromagnetism in the Distorted Honeycomb Compound KCuIn(PO4)2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21868)
*S. Gayen,S. S. Ali,V. K. Singh,B. Koteswararao,S. K. Panda*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文结合实验测量、第一性原理计算和量子蒙特卡洛模拟，系统研究了扭曲蜂窝状晶格化合物KCuInP₂O₈的电子结构与磁性质，揭示了其作为由耦合自旋链构成的准二维量子反铁磁体的物理图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解扭曲蜂窝状晶格化合物KCuInP₂O₈的电子结构与磁性质，并建立从电子结构、交换相互作用到集体磁行为的一致性理论框架。

Method: 采用GGA+U框架下的密度泛函理论（density functional theory）计算、基于磁力定理（magnetic force theorem）的微观交换作用评估，以及大规模量子蒙特卡洛（quantum Monte Carlo）模拟。

Result: KCuInP₂O₈为间接带隙绝缘体，具有Cu²⁺局域磁矩和自旋轨道耦合（spin orbit coupling）导致的有限磁晶各向异性；交换作用呈现显著层级结构，次近邻相互作用主导近邻交换，层间耦合可忽略；该交换层级自然地对应到嵌入扭曲蜂窝晶格中的弱耦合反铁磁自旋链。

Conclusion: KCuInP₂O₈是一种由耦合自旋链构成的准二维量子反铁磁体（quantum antiferromagnet），其理论计算与实验行为定量一致，为理解低维量子磁性提供了自洽的理论框架。

Abstract: We investigate the electronic structure and magnetic properties of the distorted honeycomb lattice compound KCuInP2O8 through a combination of experimental measurements, first principles calculations and quantum monte carlo simulations. Density functional theory calculations within the GGA+U framework establishes KCuInP2O8 as an indirect gap insulator with Cu2+ local moments and finite magnetocrystalline anisotropy arising from spin orbit coupling. A microscopic evaluation of magnetic exchange interactions using the magnetic force theorem reveals a pronounced hierarchy of couplings, with the next nearest neighbor interaction dominating over the nearest neighbor exchange, while interlayer couplings remain negligible. This exchange hierarchy naturally maps the system onto weakly coupled antiferromagnetic spin chains embedded in a distorted honeycomb lattice. Motivated by the ab initio estimated exchange interactions, we construct an effective spin half Hamiltonian and investigate its magnetic response using large scale quantum Monte Carlo simulations. The calculated temperature dependent susceptibility and field dependent magnetization quantitatively reproduce the experimental behavior and capture key signatures of low dimensional quantum magnetism, including a broad susceptibility maximum and a field induced saturation at low temperatures. Our results establish KCuInP2O8 as a quasi-two-dimensional quantum antiferromagnet composed of coupled spin chains, providing a consistent theoretical framework that links electronic structure, exchange interactions, and collective magnetic behavior.

</details>


### [127] [Orbital Embedding and the Physical Definition of Quantum Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21882)
*Chang-geun Oh,Shuichi Murakami*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文针对量子几何张量（Quantum Geometric Tensor）在标准计算中存在的原胞约定（unit-cell convention）模糊性问题，提出了一种通过协变导数（covariant derivative）定义的、不依赖于约定的物理QGT，并揭示了标准k·p有效理论在键序能隙系统中的失效，最后提出了几何工程（geometric engineering）这一独立调控几何响应的设计范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子几何张量（Quantum Geometric Tensor, QGT）是现代凝聚态物理（condensed matter physics）中的核心概念，但其标准计算方法——通过对布洛赫投影子（Bloch projector）求k导数——在原胞约定的选择上存在根本性歧义，特别是在处理晶胞内轨道位置（intra-cell orbital positions）时是否包含轨道相位因子e^{ikx_α}的问题上不一致。本文旨在解决这一模糊性。

Method: 通过引入一个显式包含完整位置算符（full position operator）的协变导数（covariant derivative），定义了一个不依赖于原胞约定的物理QGT。从Peierls替换（Peierls substitution）的微观推导出发，论证该形式是物理电流（physical current）的唯一必然要求。

Result: 发现了标准的k·p有效理论（k·p effective theory）在具有键序能隙（bond-ordered gaps）的系统中存在首阶（leading-order）失效，表明在相关应用中需要谨慎。此外，提出了几何工程（geometric engineering）作为一种新的设计范式，可以在不改变能量色散（energy dispersion）的情况下独立调控几何响应（geometric responses）。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个约定无关的物理QGT定义，澄清了原胞约定带来的计算歧义。同时指出了标准k·p理论在特定系统中的局限性，并提出了几何工程作为未来调控几何响应的新方向。

Abstract: The Quantum Geometric Tensor, encompassing the quantum metric and Berry curvature, is a central concept in modern condensed matter physics. However, its standard calculation via $k$-derivatives of the Bloch projector conceals a fundamental ambiguity regarding the choice of unit-cell convention, specifically in the treatment of intra-cell orbital positions (i.e., with or without the orbital position $e^{ikx_α}$). We resolve this inconsistency by introducing a convention-independent physical QGT defined via a covariant derivative that explicitly incorporates the full position operator. We demonstrate that this formulation is uniquely mandated by the microscopic derivation of the physical current via the Peierls substitution. Notably, we uncover a leading-order failure in standard $k \cdot p$ effective theories for systems with bond-ordered gaps, identifying a need for caution in their application. Finally, we propose geometric engineering as a new design paradigm, enabling the independent tuning of geometric responses without altering the energy dispersion.

</details>


### [128] [Zero-field magnetic structure of the antiferromagnetic metal EuSnP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22245)
*Mizuki Urai,Hiraku Saito,Daisuke Nishio-Hamane,Taro Nakajima,Rina Takagi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过单晶中子衍射实验，确定了反铁磁金属EuSnP在零场下的磁结构为共线A型反铁磁序，传播矢量为(0,0,1/2)，并发现Eu²⁺磁矩在Eu-P层内呈铁磁排列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解反铁磁金属EuSnP的微观磁结构，为其复杂的宏观磁行为（如多级变磁转变和磁转变温度对压力的强依赖性）提供微观基础。

Method: 采用单晶中子衍射（single-crystal neutron diffraction）实验，测量零场条件下的磁结构，并通过磁结构分析确定磁序类型与磁矩排列方式。

Result: 磁有序态的磁传播矢量为(0,0,1/2)，表明该材料实现的是共度（commensurate）反铁磁序；磁结构为共线A型反铁磁结构（collinear A-type antiferromagnetic structure），Eu²⁺磁矩在Eu-P层内呈铁磁排列。

Conclusion: 该发现为解释EuSnP在反铁磁态中的多级变磁转变以及磁转变温度对压力的强依赖性提供了微观基础。

Abstract: We investigated the zero-field magnetic structure of the antiferromagnetic metal EuSnP through single-crystal neutron diffraction experiments. The magnetic propagation vector in the magnetically ordered phase was determined to be $(0,0,1/2)$, indicating that commensurate antiferromagnetic ordering is realized in this material. The detailed magnetic structure analysis revealed a collinear A-type antiferromagnetic structure with ferromagnetic alignment of Eu$^{2+}$ moments within the Eu--P layer. The present findings provide a microscopic basis for understanding the intriguing magnetic behaviors of EuSnP, as exemplified by the multiple metamagnetic transitions in the antiferromagnetic state and the strong pressure dependence of the magnetic transition temperature.

</details>


### [129] [Quasiparticle interference as a tool to study quantum materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22487)
*Luke C. Rhodes,Yuhki Kohsaka,Tetsuo Hanaguri,Carolina de Almeida Marques,Peter Wahl*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文综述了通过扫描隧道显微镜（STM）中的准粒子干涉（QPI）技术来探测量子材料低能电子结构的方法，涵盖其原理、实验检测、物理见解及未来发展方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子材料（quantum materials）的低能电子结构（low energy electronic structure）对于控制和设计材料性能至关重要，但现有的实验技术（如ARPES）在零磁场、仅占居态等方面存在局限，因此需要更全面的探测手段。

Method: 论文综述了利用扫描隧道显微镜（scanning tunneling microscopy, STM）成像准粒子干涉（quasiparticle interference, QPI）的技术：STM在实空间成像中通过QPI获得电子结构信息，结合近年来的理论进展实现对QPI数据的常规建模（routine modelling），从而解释复杂实验数据。

Result: STM可实现优于100 μeV的能量分辨率，在低于50 mK的温度和磁场下工作，通过QPI技术在过去数十年中已成功研究多种量子材料中的关联电子行为（correlated electron behaviour）。

Conclusion: QPI技术为理解量子材料的电子结构提供了关键工具，未来仍有进一步发展的方向；该综述总结了其起源、检测方法和所获得的物理洞见。

Abstract: To understand the properties of quantum materials a detailed knowledge of the material's low energy electronic structure is key. Details of the electronic structure drive the ground state through electronic instabilities, electronic correlation effects, new electronic orders or just the absence of electronic states near the Fermi energy - making a realistic and detailed understanding crucial to be able to control and design properties of quantum materials. The past 25 years have seen a significant improvement in experimental techniques to observe the true electronic structure, in particular in techniques such as Angle resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) where energy resolutions of 2meV are routinely achievable now, which however is limited to zero magnetic field and only provides information about the occupied states. Scanning tunneling microscopy (STM) achieves a significantly better energy resolution <100$μ$eV and can operate at temperatures well below 50mK and in magnetic fields. While per se a real-space technique, by imaging quasiparticle interference (QPI) STM can also provide information about the electronic structure. This technique has been used over the past decades to study a wide range of quantum materials to understand correlated electron behaviour. Recent theoretical progress now enables routine modelling of QPI, a key requirement to interpret the complex data. Here, we review the principles of QPI, its origin, experimental detection, and the physical insight gained from the study of QPI and possible future directions for this technique.

</details>


### [130] [Exactness of Symmetry-Broken Self-Interaction Correction in the Strongly-Correlated or Classical Limit: Harmonium as a Demonstration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22488)
*Cody Woods,Yunzhi Li,Wenqing Yao,Chen Li,John Perdew*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文证明，对于谐波模型（harmonium）这一可精确求解的体系，通过对称性破缺（symmetry breaking）的Perdew-Zunger 1981自相互作用修正（self-interaction correction），可以在普朗克常数趋于零的强关联极限（strongly-correlated limit）下精确描述基态能量，并在物理值范围内保持有用精度；同时通过球面平均恢复对称的基态密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强电子关联（strong electron correlation）对波函数理论和密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）均构成重要挑战。标准密度泛函在经典极限（classical limit）下表现严重不佳，该极限即量子力学的强关联极限。因此，需要检验对称性破缺的Perdew-Zunger自相互作用修正能否在这一极限下提供精确描述。

Method: 以可精确求解的谐波模型（两个库仑相互作用电子受球对称谐振子外势束缚）为测试体系，分析普朗克常数从零到物理值（原子单位下为1）区间内，对称性破缺的Perdew-Zunger 1981自相互作用修正的表现。通过球面平均（spherical averaging）恢复依赖于普朗克常数的对称基态密度。

Result: 在普朗克常数趋于零的极限下，对称性破缺的自相互作用修正对谐波模型的基态能量是精确的；在0到物理值之间的所有取值区间内，该修正均保持有用的计算精度。球面平均操作可以恢复对称的基态密度。

Conclusion: 对称性破缺的Perdew-Zunger自相互作用修正能够正确描述强关联极限下的基态能量，并且在从经典极限到物理值的连续参数范围内均有效。这为改进密度泛函理论处理强电子关联问题提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Strong electron correlation is an important challenge to both wavefunction and density functional theory. It has been argued that the Perdew-Zunger 1981 self-interaction correction to any density functional approximation, after symmetry breaking, can correctly describe the ground-state energy in the strongly-correlated limit in which each electron is described by a highly-localized and non-overlapped one-electron spin orbital. It has also been argued that the classical limit, in which Planck's constant tends to zero, is the strongly-correlated limit of quantum mechanics, where standard density functionals fail badly, as demonstrated by the exactly-solvable problem of harmonium (two Coulomb-interacting electrons bound by a spherically-symmetric harmonic-oscillator external potential). Here we combine these two ideas and demonstrate that, for harmonium, symmetry-broken self-interaction correction is exact in the limit where Planck's constant tends to zero, and usefully accurate for all values between 0 and the physical value (1 in atomic units). We also show that the Planck-constant-dependent symmetric ground-state density can be restored by spherical averaging of the broken-symmetry density.

</details>
